While red teaming strategies help mitigate specific risks, broader concerns persist regarding linguistic constraints, biases, and the sycophantic tendencies of LLMs. This chapter explores how LLMs can be used to significantly scale up and democratise deliberation, particularly in fostering inclusivity and empowering traditionally marginalised groups. Drawing on concepts from Systemic-Functional Linguistics, the chapter examines how variations across language users (for example, with respect to socio-demographic groups) and across language use (for example, with respect to communicative functions) shape participation in AI-supported deliberation. The chapter presents AI-driven deliberation studies and assesses their potential to scaffold argumentation, enhance access, and reduce the influence of exclusionary linguistic norms and biases which are embedded in prestigious registers. At the same time, the chapter cautions against both overclaiming, which leads to unrealistic expectations, and underclaiming, which risks missed opportunities for AI-assisted engagement. The chapter concludes by identifying future research directions to maximise the democratic potential of AI-assisted participation while embedding ethical safeguards to counteract the reproduction of linguistic inequalities.

</details>


### [38] [REDACT: A Systematically Controlled Multilingual Benchmark for Personal Information Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19881)
*Guneesh Vats,Anubha Agrawal,Shikha Singhal,Ajita Dash,Praison Selvaraj,Vidhan Jhawar,Ranga Prasad Chenna,Bharadwaj Y M G*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REDACT 是一个大规模系统化 PII 基准测试，通过 9 个控制维度和敏感度分级评估，揭示了传统规则式探测器在高敏感度数据上的性能欠佳，并证实了 LLM 在处理复杂 PII 识别任务时的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 PII 检测基准在实体类型覆盖、生成条件科学性以及故障分析方面存在局限，难以准确评估哪些表面特征会导致 PII 检测器失败。

Method: 构建了一个包含 13,427 条记录、涵盖 51 种实体类型和 25 种语言的控制型多语言基准测试（REDACT）。使用 strength-2 覆盖数组采样器控制 9 个生成轴（如领域、格式、难度等），并引入包含披露状态、披露形式和 GDPR 敏感度分级的元数据进行分层评估。通过对比基于规则的系统（Presidio）与先进的 LLM 模型（GLiNER, OpenAI Privacy Filter, GPT-4.1, Claude 3.5 Sonnet）进行基准验证。

Result: 实验发现汇总的 F1 分数掩盖了模型之间的结构化差异。基于规则的探测器在处理高风险（HIGH 敏感度）类别时表现极差（召回率仅 0.07），而 LLM 架构在这些类别上表现更佳。LLM-as-judge 评估进一步证实，正确识别数据敏感度分级是该任务中最具挑战性的环节。

Conclusion: REDACT 揭示了现有 PII 检测器在处理高敏感度数据和复杂披露形式时的架构依赖性缺陷。LLM（特别是 GPT-4.1 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6）在处理高敏感度 PII 时比传统的基于规则的方法更稳健，该基准测试为评估 PII 检测性能和改进敏感度感知能力提供了更深层的工具。

Abstract: Benchmark infrastructure for personally identifiable information (PII) detection remains limited: existing corpora cover few entity types, use ad hoc generation conditions, and do not show which surface conditions cause detector failures. We present REDACT, a systematically controlled multilingual PII benchmark with 13,427 records, 324,078 entity annotations, 51 entity types, 4,127 surface-form patterns, and 25 languages across 9 scripts. A strength-2 covering-array sampler controls nine generation axes: domain, format, difficulty, length, density, code-switching, language, adjacency, and co-occurrence. Three entity-level metadata fields (disclosure status, disclosure form, and a GDPR-aligned sensitivity tier) enable stratified evaluation beyond aggregate or per-type F1. From the full benchmark, we evaluate five detectors (Presidio, GLiNER, the OpenAI Privacy Filter, GPT-4.1, and Claude Sonnet 4.6) on a locked, language-stratified sample of 1,000 records. Aggregate F1 masks an architecture-dependent failure structure: the rule-based detector performs poorly on the highest-stakes data, including HIGH-sensitivity categories (recall 0.07) and non-verbatim disclosure forms, while the LLM detectors remain more robust, with the HIGH tier as their strongest sensitivity slice. A three-model reference-free LLM-as-judge assessment corroborates that sensitivity-tier assignment is the task's hardest axis. We release the benchmark, schema, prompts, and stratified evaluation harness.

</details>


### [39] [Light-weight Pronunciation Assessment via Discrete Speech Token Surprisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19910)
*Syeda Faiza Ahmed Sara,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需大量标注L2数据的自动化发音评估轻量级框架，通过结合母语语音的离散化表征、基于语言模型的惊喜度指标和文本对齐特征，实现了接近监督学习方法的评估精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动化发音评估方法严重依赖昂贵且难以获取的带标注学习者错误语料库，亟需开发低成本、利用母语资源即可实现的高效评估框架。

Method: 1. 使用无监督的SSL编码器和K-means码本将语音离散化；
2. 利用在母语语料上训练的标记语言模型（Token LM）计算惊喜度（Surprisal），用于捕捉音位偏差；
3. 引入基于Text2DUnit-DTW的转录引导模块，负责从参考文本预测母语标记序列并进行比对；
4. 将惊喜度与对齐特征通过简单的回归模型进行融合。

Result: 在SpeechOcean762数据集上，PCC（皮尔逊相关系数）从0.60提升至0.66，接近监督学习基线；在L2-ARCTIC数据集上的跨数据集评估中表现出稳定的一致性提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种仅需少量或无需标注学习者数据的自动化发音评估框架，通过结合基于SSL表征的惊喜度（Surprisal）分析和文本对齐技术，显著提升了评估性能，且具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Training automated pronunciation assessment often relies on labeled learner errors or non-native corpora that are costly to collect. We propose a lightweight framework trained only on native speech resources, operating unsupervised or lightly calibrated with a small set of scored utterances. At inference, learner speech is discretized with an SSL encoder and a K-means codebook. A token language model trained on native sequences computes surprisal where higher surprisal indicates phonotactic deviation. We add a transcript-guided Text2DUnit--DTW module that predicts native token sequences from reference text and aligns them to acoustic tokens to derive error-sensitive features. Surprisal and alignment features are fused via simple regression. On SpeechOcean762, PCC improves from 0.60 to 0.66 with transcript guidance, near supervised baselines. Cross-dataset evaluation on L2-ARCTIC shows consistent gains.

</details>


### [40] [GEMS: Geometric Constraints Enable Multi-Semantic Superposition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19946)
*Yu Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEMS 是一种训练无关的激活操纵方法，通过解决“分布偏移”和“方向干扰”这两个导致模型崩溃的核心问题，实现了模型多维度的稳定并发操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活操纵方法多针对单方向，在进行多语义方向叠加时会导致模型发生“崩溃”（accuracy collapse），现有研究缺乏对该现象背后机制的深入解析及应对方案。

Method: 提出 GEMS（Geometric Steering）方法，包含两个核心机制：1. 针对分布偏移的范数保持加权叠加与注意力路径注入；2. 针对方向干扰的实时正交化处理。

Result: 在 GSM8K 测试中，三路并发注入下模型准确率保持在 98%（无约束叠加仅为 4%）；在 Wikitext-2 上困惑度仅上升 2.2%。验证了该方法在不同规模模型（3B-31B）间的通用性。

Conclusion: GEMS 框架通过几何约束（范数保持加权叠加和实时正交化）有效解决了多向量激活操纵时的模型崩溃问题，证明了在无需重训练的前提下实现多维度指令注入的可行性。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states at inference time without retraining. Existing methods handle only single-direction injection; when multiple semantic directions are superposed without constraints, the model collapses. We show that this collapse decomposes into two independently acting sources: distributional deviation, where additive perturbations accumulate in norm across layers and drive activations outside the training distribution, and directional interference, where non-orthogonal semantic vectors mutually dampen when superposed. These two sources define the design constraints that any training-free multi-directional intervention must address. As one instantiation of these principles, we propose GEMS, a training-free method that maps each source to a corresponding geometric constraint: norm-preserving weighted superposition and targeted attention-pathway injection for distributional deviation, and real-time orthogonalization for directional interference. On GSM8K, injecting three concurrent non-mathematical directions preserves accuracy at 98% (baseline 92%), while unconstrained addition collapses to 4%; on Wikitext-2, the same injection incurs only 2.2% PPL increase. Component ablation isolates the causal role of each constraint, and layer-level probes confirm that orthogonalized signals survive the FFN pathway and reach the output distribution with semantic specificity. Qualitative steering effects transfer across architectures from 3B to 31B.

</details>


### [41] [Source-Grounded Data Generation for Text-to-JSON Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20072)
*Sunghee Ahn,Guijin Son,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长文档转 JSON 数据稀缺问题，本文提出 STAGE 框架，利用表格数据作为溯源基准，通过 LLM 生成高质量、可验证的训练样本，大幅提升了模型的信息抽取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行业存在大量长篇非结构化文档，难以转化为机器可读的结构化数据（如 JSON）；现有方法在构建高质量、可扩展的文本转 JSON 训练数据时面临挑战。

Method: 提出 STAGE（Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration）管道，利用 LLM 基于底层电子表格（Spreadsheet）规模化合成报告及其对应的 JSON Schema，并进行强制性的溯源验证以确保 ground-truth 的准确性。

Result: 在 STAGE-Eval 基准测试中表现优异，Qwen3-4B 模型的精确匹配指标（Exact Match）从 31.37% 提升至 74.27%，值准确率（Value Accuracy）从 45.46% 提升至 90.69%。

Conclusion: STAGE 提供了一种高效且可靠的文本转 JSON 数据生成范式，通过表格溯源验证确保了数据的准确性，显著提升了模型的信息抽取能力，为处理长篇、非结构化文档的数据集构建提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: From financial filings to clinical records, legacy industries rely heavily on long, unstructured documents to store high-value information. Reliably extracting this information into structured, machine-readable representations is a key prerequisite to making the contents accessible to automated systems. JSON is a natural target for such structured extraction, yet constructing reliable and scalable text-to-JSON training data remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose STAGE (Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration), a source-grounded data generation pipeline that constructs reports and JSON schema by using LLMs for scalable synthesis while validating ground-truth values against the underlying spreadsheet. Evaluations on STAGE-Eval, our source-grounded benchmark with an 851-example test set, show that STAGE produces stronger training data than existing approaches. This improves Qwen3-4B exact match from 31.37% to 74.27% and value accuracy from 45.46% to 90.69%.

</details>


### [42] [IHUBERT: Vector-Based Semantic Deduplication and Domain-Balanced Pretraining for Persian Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20089)
*Arash Ghafouri,Mahdi Firouzmandi,Hossein Saberi,Mohammad Reza Hasani Ahangar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对波斯语预训练语料质量不佳及评估单一的问题，IHUBERT 通过优化数据预处理和分词方案，训练出了一个在多项 NLU 任务（特别是问答与推理）表现优异的单语言预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语预训练模型（PLMs）面临高质量大规模语料匮乏的问题，且现有评估主要集中于简单的分类和 NER 任务，缺乏对复杂理解任务的覆盖。

Method: 1. 模型架构：基于 RoBERTa-base (125M) 从头训练；2. 数据预处理：多阶段清洗（去重、匿名化、语义去重、领域均衡控制）；3. Tokenization：训练了 139k 词表大小的 BPE 分词器，以优化波斯语表示；4. 评估：在覆盖 NER、情感分析、NLI、问答、关系抽取等 7 个任务上进行广泛评估。

Result: IHUBERT 在多项任务中取得突破，尤其在 PQuAD、ParsiNLU-RC 的问答任务以及 FarsTail 推理任务中达到领先水平，证明了语料库精选和针对性分词对波斯语模型的重要性。

Conclusion: IHUBERT 通过高质量、大规模的语料库构建和针对波斯语形态优化的 tokenizer 设计，显著提升了波斯语在长文本理解（如 QA）任务中的表现，为波斯语预训练模型提供了更有效的方法论。

Abstract: Persian pretrained language models (PLMs) are still limited by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality pretraining corpora and by insufficient evaluation beyond standard classification and NER tasks. We present IHUBERT, a monolingual Persian PLM trained from scratch with the RoBERTa-base encoder (125M parameters) on a 45 GB curated subset of the Sepahr-Danesh collection (about 7-8B tokens). To improve corpus quality and reduce redundancy, we employ a multi-stage preprocessing pipeline that includes normalization, exact and near-duplicate removal, anonymization, and vector-database-based semantic deduplication for distribution balancing control across domains and registers. We additionally train a 139k-vocabulary BPE tokenizer on the full pretraining corpus to better capture Persian morphology and orthographic variation. IHUBERT is evaluated on seven Persian NLU benchmarks covering NER, sentiment analysis, topic classification, NLI, extractive question answering, and relation extraction, using task-standard metrics (entity-level F1, Macro-F1, EM/F1). IHUBERT achieves its strongest gains on extractive QA, ranking first on both PQuAD (F1 88.3542) and ParsiNLU-RC (F1 49.0987), and attains the best result on FarsTail (Macro-F1 0.8350). On NER and topic classification, it remains competitive (e.g., 0.8308 F1 on ParsTwiNER; 0.7953 Macro-F1 on DigiMag), while relation extraction remains the main remaining gap (0.6684 Macro-F1 on PERLEX). A controlled tokenizer ablation on the IHUBERT pretraining corpus shows that BPE yields slightly lower subword fragmentation than WordPiece at matched vocabulary size, supporting our tokenization design. Overall, IHUBERT advances Persian language modeling through semantically curated large-scale pretraining and broad evaluation across both classification and comprehension-oriented tasks.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Preference Is Weak or Absent in Verifiable Instruction-Following Revision: A Four-Model Test Under Genuine Authorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20093)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证分析发现，大语言模型在被要求修正自身存在的指令遵循错误时，并不表现出针对自身输出的偏袒或拒不纠错倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“自我偏好”研究指出模型倾向于偏爱自身生成的内容，引发了研究者对模型是否会抗拒对其错误文本进行有效修正的担忧。本研究旨在通过确定性的验证机制测试这一假设。

Method: 使用 IFEval 基准测试，由官方规则检查器确定草稿的约束违规情况及候选修正方案。比较“原作者模型”与“中立评估模型”对同一修正方案的接纳率，评估模型是否存在拒绝修正自身错误的倾向。共测试了 4 个中等规模模型家族，包含 85 组对比。

Result: 作者模型与中立模型在拒绝已验证纠错时的比率没有显著差异（差距为 -5.1 个百分点，95% CI 为 [-12.9, +2.7]），未观察到显著的自我偏好。在拒绝修正时，97% 的解释集中在纠错逻辑而非主观偏好。

Conclusion: 在当前实验规模下，没有证据表明模型对自身生成的文本存在抗拒修正的“自我偏好”倾向。模型在拒绝经过验证的正确修正时，主要理由是基于内容的纠错，而非偏见。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly review and revise text, including their own. A documented self-preference bias (models favoring their own generations when acting as judges) raises the question of whether models also resist valid corrections to their own writing. We test this in a setting where "valid" is decided not by another model but by a deterministic verifier: instruction-following revision on IFEval. A model writes a draft; the official IFEval checker confirms the draft violates a constraint and that a candidate edit fixes it; the model then accepts or rejects that edit either as the genuine in-context author or as a fresh model that sees the draft neutrally. Across four mid-tier model families and 85 author-versus-fresh comparisons, we find no detectable self-preference: authors reject verified-good fixes to their own drafts at essentially the same rate as fresh models judging the same drafts (gap -5.1 pp, 95% CI [-12.9, +2.7]). A self-skepticism hint from a smaller pilot did not replicate at scale. The one robust observation is qualitative: when authors do reject a verified-good fix, 97% of their stated reasons are flaw-catching rather than preference, that is, about the character of rejections, not an elevated rate. Effects smaller than ~13 pp cannot be excluded at this sample size.

</details>


### [44] [HydraHead: From Head-Level Functional Heterogeneity to Specialized Attention Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097)
*Zhentao Tan,Wei Chen,Jingyi Shen,Yao Liu,Xu Shen,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HydraHead 提出了一种在“注意力头”维度混合全注意力和线性注意力的架构，利用可解释性筛选关键头，在显著降低长文本处理计算复杂度的同时，实现了卓越的上下文扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的二次复杂度限制了长文本处理，且现有的层级混合设计未能充分挖掘不同注意力头的功能异质性与空间，导致混合效率不足。

Method: 1. 可解释性分析：发现同一层内各注意力头具有功能异质性。
2. 架构创新：提出 HydraHead，将全注意力（FA）和线性注意力（LA）在“头（Head）”维度进行混合。
3. 关键技术：包含（1）基于可解释性的关键头提取策略（仅对检索关键头保留 FA）；（2）尺度归一化融合模块（解决 FA 和 LA 的分布差异）。
4. 训练策略：采用参数重用与蒸馏的三阶段迁移流程。

Result: HydraHead 在仅使用 15B tokens 训练的情况下，在 512K 上下文长度上相比基线提升超 69%，不仅优于其他混合模型，还达到了与同规模主流模型（如 Qwen3.5）相当的水平，且 LA:FA 比率达到了 7:1 的高效率。

Conclusion: HydraHead 证明了在注意力头维度进行细粒度混合（而非传统的层级混合）是提升长上下文处理能力的高效路径。实验表明，该方法在大幅节省计算资源的同时，能达到甚至超越主流大模型的上下文处理性能，证明了头级混合架构具备强大的扩展潜力。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention poses a critical bottleneck for long-context processing, spurring interest in hybrid attention designs. Most open-source hybrid models adopt a layer-wise strategy. Yet, prior work has noted the inherent difficulty of integrating Linear Attention (LA) with Full Attention (FA), suggesting that the design space of attention hybridization remains underexplored. To probe this space, we conduct interpretability analysis and observe that layers exhibit block-wise functional similarity, while individual heads within the same layer display distinct functional specialization despite sharing input features. This head-level heterogeneity suggests that the head dimension provides a natural and principled granularity for fusing heterogeneous attention signals. Building on this insight, we introduce HydraHead, a novel architecture that hybridizes FA and LA along the head axis. HydraHead features two key innovations: (1) an interpretability-driven selection strategy that identifies retrieval-critical heads and preserves FA only for them, and (2) a scale-normalized fusion module that reconciles the distributional gap between FA and LA head outputs. By leveraging a three-stage transfer pipeline with parameter reuse and distillation, we achieve high-performance hybrid models with minimal training overhead. Under a unified training setup, HydraHead outperforms other hybrid designs in long-context tasks while maintaining strong general reasoning. With interpretability-driven head selection, it matches a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid's long-context performance at a 7:1 LA-to-FA ratio. Crucially, trained on only 15B tokens, HydraHead achieves over 69% improvement over the baseline at 512K context length, approaching Qwen3.5, a leading model of comparable size with a native context length of 256K. This highlights the significant scaling potential of head-level hybridization.

</details>


### [45] [From Texts to Scores: Tracing the Emergence of Essay Quality Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20152)
*Jiaxu Zuo,Mu You,Kaixin Lan,Tao Fang,Yujia Huo,Henghua Shen,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLMs）在作文评分任务中能够线性地编码文章质量，并首次识别出能够直接影响评分结果的“关键神经元”，为基于LLM的自动评分系统提供了深度可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在自动作文评分（AES）领域应用广泛，但其内部是如何评估文章质量的“黑盒”机制尚不明确，缺乏对其内部表征和决策过程的系统研究。

Method: 研究对8种LLM在3个数据集（ASAP++, CSEE, ENEM）上的隐层表征进行了系统分析。方法包括：1. 线性/非线性探针（Probing）；2. 跨提示词泛化测试；3. 维度缩减分析；4. 神经元层面的归因分析与干预实验。

Result: 1. 文章质量信息以线性可解码形式存在于LLM表征中；2. 这种表征随层数加深而逐渐显现并具鲁棒性；3. 发现了个体“文章评分神经元”，其活跃度与分数显著相关；4. 神经元分布与文章长度相关，长文章更多依赖深层信息。

Conclusion: 研究表明大型语言模型（LLMs）能够以线性可解码的形式编码文章质量信息，且这种编码具有跨提示词的通用性和逻辑结构。此外，通过识别并定位“评分神经元”，研究验证了LLM内部存在专门负责评估文章质量的机制，为理解LLM在自动作文评分（AES）中的表现提供了可解释性依据。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have substantially transformed Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet the internal mechanisms underlying LLM-based scoring remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically analyze the hidden representations of eight LLMs across two English essay datasets (ASAP++, CSEE) and one Portuguese dataset (ENEM). Using linear probing, cross-prompt generalization, dimensionality reduction, and neuron-level analyses, we find consistent evidence that essay quality information is encoded in a linearly accessible form within LLM representations. These representations emerge progressively across layers, remain robust across prompting strategies, and partially transfer across essay prompts despite differences in scoring rubrics. In addition, nonlinear probes provide only marginal and inconsistent improvements over linear probes, suggesting that most essay quality information is already linearly decodable. We further identify individual ``essay scoring neurons'' whose activations strongly correlate with essay scores and whose behavior is sensitive to targeted intervention. Moreover, the layer-wise distribution of these neurons systematically shifts with essay length, with longer essays relying more heavily on deeper layers. Overall, our findings provide evidence that LLMs encode structured representations related to essay quality and offer new insights into the interpretability of LLM-based AES systems.

</details>


### [46] [MedRLM: Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence for Long-Context Clinical Reasoning, Sensor-Guided Screening, Evidence-Grounded Decision Support, and Community-to-Tertiary Referral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20164)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedRLM 是一种针对长序列、多模态临床数据的递归智能协作框架，通过动态检索、证据图谱关联和传感器驱动的推理触发，解决了传统单一提示词在复杂临床决策中表现脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 和 RAG 系统依赖单步推理，难以应对临床决策中复杂的、长周期的异构数据（如 EHR、影像、传感器信号、临床指南）及动态的转诊约束。

Method: 提出 MedRLM 递归多模态临床智能框架：1. 采用递归检查与检索机制，而非简单的单步提示；2. 设计了协调文本、EHR、影像、传感器等专业 Agent 的协同机制；3. 构建了“临床证据图谱记忆”（Clinical Evidence Graph Memory）；4. 引入基于传感器数据的递归触发机制与不确定性门控优化。

Result: 设计了一套涵盖 EHR、放射影像、ECG 及 ICU 时序数据的多模态评估方案，旨在提升复杂临床场景下的推理能力，并增强临床决策的可解释性与可靠性。

Conclusion: MedRLM 将医疗 AI 从传统的“静态问答”模式转变为一种“可审计、多模态、感知工作流”的临床决策支持系统，证明了递归式 Agent 协作框架在处理长序列、异构临床数据推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world clinical decision support requires reasoning over heterogeneous and longitudinal patient information rather than answering isolated medical questions. However, current medical large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems often rely on single-step prompting or retrieval, which can be fragile when clinical evidence is distributed across long electronic health records, medical images, sensor streams, guidelines, and referral constraints. This paper proposes MedRLM, a Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence framework for long-context clinical reasoning, sensor-guided screening, and community-to-tertiary referral support. Instead of compressing all patient information into one prompt, MedRLM treats the patient case as an external clinical environment that can be recursively inspected, decomposed, retrieved, verified, and synthesized. The framework coordinates specialized agents for clinical text, longitudinal EHR, medical imaging, physiological sensor signals, guideline retrieval, uncertainty auditing, and referral planning. It further introduces a Clinical Evidence Graph Memory to connect patient-specific observations with retrieved evidence, standardized definitions, sensor-derived biomarkers, and referral criteria. A sensor-guided recursive triggering mechanism activates deeper reasoning when abnormal physiological or behavioral patterns are detected, while uncertainty-gated refinement supports clinician review for high-risk or low-confidence cases. We also outline a real-data evaluation design using public and credentialed clinical datasets spanning EHR, radiology, ECG, ICU time series, and referral-proxy outcomes. MedRLM aims to move medical AI from static question answering toward auditable, multimodal, and workflow-aware clinical decision support.

</details>


### [47] [ReNikud: Audio-Supervised Hebrew Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20179)
*Maxim Melichov,Yakov Kolani,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReNikud 提出了一种利用大规模音频弱监督和字符对齐架构的现代希伯来语 G2P 转换方法，无需人工标注即可实现准确的口语化音素预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希伯来语的“辅音文字”（Abjad）特性导致书写时元音缺失，产生极大的拼读歧义；传统方法依赖劳动密集型的元音标注（Nikud），且难以准确反映非正式的口语发音或音标特征。

Method: 1. 弱监督学习：利用基于音素的自动语音识别（ASR）管道，从大规模无标注希伯来语音频中生成伪标签，捕捉真实的口语发音模式。2. 伪元音化架构：采用一种序列到序列的架构，强制在每个字符位置预测 IPA 音素，利用希伯来语书写系统（Abjad）的字符对齐特性作为归纳偏置。

Result: 在既有的希伯来语 G2P Benchmark 及新设立的针对口语的 MILIM Benchmark 上，ReNikud 均超越了现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: ReNikud 成功克服了现代希伯来语 G2P 转换中缺乏带元音标注数据及口语处理困难的瓶颈，通过弱监督音频学习和强制字符对齐架构，在基准测试中优于当前最先进方法，为希伯来语语音技术提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion for Modern Hebrew is needed for applications like text-to-speech (TTS), but is challenging due to the language's abjad writing system, which leaves vowels largely unwritten, creating substantial ambiguity. Standard approaches first predict vowel diacritics (nikud) to produce International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA) transcriptions, but this is limited: vocalization data is scarce and laborious to produce, it does not specify features such as lexical stress, and it reflects formal grammatical rules rather than everyday spoken pronunciation. Direct sequence-to-sequence IPA prediction, meanwhile, struggles on limited data and fails to exploit the character-level alignment characteristic of abjads. Our method, ReNikud, overcomes these limitations with two key insights: (1) Weak audio supervision via a phoneme-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) pseudo-labeling pipeline on thousands of hours of unlabeled Hebrew audio, yielding phonemic transcriptions that reflect natural spoken norms without manual annotation. (2) A pseudo-vocalization architecture that predicts IPA phonemes at each character position, enforcing character-level alignment as an inductive bias. Results on existing Hebrew G2P benchmarks and the new targeted MILIM benchmark for spoken Hebrew show that ReNikud surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods. We will release our code and trained models to support further work on Hebrew TTS and speech technologies.

</details>


### [48] [Pitch Spelling Jazz Lead Sheets, Solo Transcriptions, Classical Piano and Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20198)
*Augustin Bouquillard,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两阶段优化（模态寻找与调性确定）的算法，能根据MIDI数据自动推断出规范的音乐记谱法（音名、调号及局部调式），并适用于爵士乐、古典乐等多种流派的音乐转录与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对MIDI格式的音高数据，解决如何自动将其转换为人眼可识别、符合乐理规范的记谱法（如确定正确的音名、调号及局部调性），以辅助音乐转录（特别是爵士乐独奏转录）、理论分析及教学。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段联合优化算法：第一阶段“模态（modal）”阶段通过最短路径搜索，为每小节确定最优局部音阶以最小化临时记号；第二阶段“调性（tonal）”阶段基于局部音阶结果，全局评估并确定最优调号和音名表示。此外，研究还定义了评估不同爵士调式之间距离的新指标。

Result: 对爵士乐谱、爵士即兴独奏、传统曲调及古典钢琴曲等多种数据集进行了验证，证明该方法能生成高质量、符合书写习惯的音乐符号表示。

Conclusion: 该算法成功实现了将MIDI音高数据转换为符合音乐理论习惯的记谱法（音名、调号及局部调式），能够有效应用于音乐转录、音乐分析及文化遗产保存等领域。算法定义的音阶距离在音乐学研究中也具潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for pitch spelling and key estimation. Given an input in MIDI-like format, containing information on note pitches (expressed in semitones relative to the lowest reference note) and bar boundaries, it estimates the appropriate note names, a global Key Signature, and a local scale for each bar. This related information elements are evaluated jointly during two stages of optimisation. During an initial 'modal' stage, a probable scale is proposed for each bar, minimising the number of accidentals to be printed in the printed score with a shortest-path search. Then, during a second stage called 'tonal', these local scales are used to estimate the Key Signature and note names that would result in the best musical notation for the entire piece. We present evaluations conducted on datasets comprising a variety of digital musical scores: jazz lead sheets taken from the Real Book, transcriptions of recordings of jazz soli and bass lines, traditional tunes, as well as classical scores for piano and monophonic instruments. Our procedure was originally designed for use in music transcription, specifically for building digital collections of jazz solos transcribed from audio recordings, for the purposes of music analysis, teaching and the preservation of cultural heritage. This method should also prove useful for other tasks related to the processing of musical notation. Furthermore, to this end, we have defined new distances between various common jazz scales, which may be of some interest to musicological studies.

</details>


### [49] [CzechDocs: A Multiway Parallel Dataset of Formatted Documents for Minority Languages in Czechia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20212)
*Josef Jon,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CzechDocs 数据集，旨在解决多语言格式化文档（HTML/DOCX/PDF）在机器翻译中的格式保留评估问题，并公开了评估工具和验证集以促进研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器翻译系统在处理文档时，往往忽视对原文档格式（如 HTML、DOCX、PDF）的保留，因此需要一个专门的基准数据集来评估和推进格式保留翻译技术。

Method: 构建了一个包含 HTML、DOCX 和 PDF 格式的多路平行语料库（CzechDocs），涵盖捷克语、乌克兰语、英语、越南语和俄语等；并对比了当前主流的格式保留机器翻译方案，且配套发布了评估工具包。

Result: 发布了 CzechDocs 数据集及相关评估工具包，展示了在当前主流格式保留方法下的性能对比，并预留了盲测集用于未来的共享任务。

Conclusion: CzechDocs 为格式保留的机器翻译研究提供了一个基准，有助于推动多语言、多格式文档翻译技术的发展。作者通过公开验证集和工具包，鼓励社区对该领域进行进一步探索。

Abstract: We present CzechDocs, a multiway parallel dataset of formatted documents (HTML, DOCX, and PDF) covering Czech and minority languages used in Czechia-primarily Ukrainian and English, with smaller portions of Vietnamese, Russian and other languages. The dataset is designed to support the evaluation of machine translation systems that aim to preserve document formatting during translation. We provide a comparison of the most common approaches to format-preserving machine translation on a validation subset of the dataset. This validation split, together with the evaluation toolkit, is publicly released for further research. A held-out test split will be reserved for a future shared task focused on document-level translation with formatting preservation.

</details>


### [50] [Actionable Activation Directions for Detecting and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment Across Language Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20225)
*Abdul Rafay Syed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型微调不安全代码导致的失调是可以被模型内部的线性方向捕捉并干预的，但这种因果方向在不同模型架构间难以直接迁移，提示未来审计应侧重模型内部 probing。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在微调不安全代码后会出现涌现式的失调（misalignment），本研究旨在探究这种失调在不同架构模型间是否有共同的、可操作的激活空间方向。

Method: 使用差值均值（difference-in-means）探测法提取激活空间中的“误导性方向”；通过因果干预（Causal steering/subtracting）评估其对减少代码泄露的影响；通过岭回归（ridge regression）评估跨架构方向迁移的有效性与特异性。

Result: 在模型内部，差值均值方向能够以99.6%的精度区分对齐与未对齐状态，因果干预可显著降低21-51个点的代码泄露；跨架构迁移虽然在行为上能抑制误导信息，但缺乏特异性（随机方向效果相当），且存在非对称的“赠予者-接收者”拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 研究发现，单一模型内部存在具有因果可操作性的线性对齐方向，但跨架构的线性迁移存在特异性局限。对于对齐审计，应优先采用针对特定模型的探测方法，而非盲目应用跨架构模型微调的修正方向。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on insecure code induces emergent misalignment with poorly understood internal structure. We investigate whether this misalignment corresponds to a causally actionable activation-space direction shared across architectures. Across four instruction-tuned model families (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B, Ministral-3-3B) finetuned identically, a difference-in-means direction achieves 99.6% separation of aligned and misaligned activations at each model's final layer. Causal steering by subtracting this direction reduces code spillover by 21-51 points, while a secure-code control confirms content specificity. Cross-architecture transfer via ridge regression maps yields large behavioral suppression (up to 46 points) but fails specificity controls as random and orthogonal directions perform comparably. We identify a two-tier specificity structure: within-model directions are causally specific and actionable; cross-model directions are causally real but non-specific. An asymmetric transfer topology emerges, with Gemma and Qwen acting as geometric donors and Llama as a receiver. These findings define the limits of linear cross-architecture correction and recommend within-model probing for auditing.

</details>


### [51] [The Register Gap: A Meaning Intelligence Framework for Nigerian Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20255)
*Celestine Achi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对AI在尼日利亚公共话语分析中因“语境缺失”导致的性能瓶颈，本文提出了九维度“意义智能框架”（MIF），证明了结构化的语境引导能显著提升模型对反讽、隐喻及深层意图的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的尼日利亚语言基准测试（如NaijaSenti、AfriSenti）仅停留在简单的三分类情感分析，无法处理尼日利亚公共话语中复杂的语境依赖，导致AI系统在处理具有特定语境和多重弦外之音的表达时失效。

Method: 1. 提出了“意义智能框架”（MIF），涵盖语体、表层情感、真实意图、反讽、隐性含义、风险分级、标注置信度、说话人情绪及传播行动建议等9个维度；2. 构建包含标准英语、尼日利亚英语、尼日利亚皮钦语和混合语种的30项标注校准数据集；3. 对比Gemini 2.5 Flash模型在零样本（Zero-shot）与MIF框架引导下的评估效果。

Result: 应用MIF框架后，模型性能显著提升：语体分类准确率从33.3%跃升至73.3%（+40个百分点）；总分从73.2提升至78.6；其中在隐性含义识别（+10分）和策略行动建议（+10.3分）方面提升最为明显。

Conclusion: 针对尼日利亚公共话语的AI系统主要失败原因在于“语境缺失”而非翻译错误。通过MIF框架进行上下文引导（Schema-informed prompting），模型在复杂语义、反讽与隐喻识别及策略建议方面的能力显著提升，验证了结构化语义分析对于提升语言模型在特定文化语境下表现的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce the Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF), a nine-dimension annotation and evaluation schema for Nigerian public discourse that separates surface sentiment from true communicative intent. Existing benchmarks for Nigerian languages, including NaijaSenti and AfriSenti, treat sentiment classification as a three-way polarity task (positive, negative, neutral). We argue that the dominant failure mode of AI systems on Nigerian discourse is not translation failure but context failure: the same utterance carries opposite pragmatic force depending on speaker, audience, and situation. The MIF operationalises this insight across nine scored dimensions: register, surface sentiment, true intent, irony, coded subtext, risk tier, annotator confidence, speaker emotion, and recommended communications action. We construct a 30-item calibration dataset spanning Standard English, Nigerian English, Nigerian Pidgin, and code-mixed registers, and evaluate a frontier language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) under zero-shot and schema-informed prompting conditions. The headline finding is the Register Gap: zero-shot register classification accuracy is 33.3%, rising to 73.3% (+40 points) when the model receives the MIF schema in-context. The composite Meaning Intelligence Score increases by 5.4 points (73.2 to 78.6) under schema-informed prompting, with the largest practical gains in register identification, coded-subtext detection (+10 points), and strategic action recommendation (+10.3 points). We release the framework specification, annotation guidelines, and the 30-item public calibration set to support reproducibility, while retaining a private holdout corpus for contamination-protected evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [PsyScore: A Psychometrically-Aware Framework for Trait-Adaptive Essay Scoring and ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20287)
*Wei Xia,Jin Wu,Haoran Shi,Xiangyu Wang,Chanjin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyScore是一个结合传统心理测量模型（IRT）与大模型反馈生成的自动化作文评分框架，通过能力参数将评分与差异化教学反馈链接在一起，解决了传统AES系统可解释性差及反馈针对性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化作文评分（AES）中，神经评分模型缺乏可解释性，而大模型（LLM）生成的反馈往往忽略了学生的能力水平。现有研究未能将评分（诊断）与教学反馈（指导）有效结合，缺乏连贯性。

Method: 提出了PsyScore框架，核心包含三个模块：1. Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer：将分级部分信用模型（GPCM）融入神经网络，实现可解释的评分；2. ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator：基于最近发展区（ZPD）理论，根据诊断出的能力参数动态生成分层反馈；3. Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation：通过成对偏好判断与学生修订模拟来评估反馈质量。

Result: 在ASAP++数据集上的实验结果表明，PsyScore不仅在评分准确性上具有竞争力，同时在反馈的教学对齐度（pedagogical alignment）和分层适应性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: PsyScore成功整合了测验心理测量学（Psychometrics）与大语言模型生成能力，通过共享潜在能力表征，实现了既具备专业评分解释力，又能根据学生水平提供差异化教学反馈的自动化评分系统，为教育科技领域解决“评分与反馈割裂”问题提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Effective Automated Essay Scoring (AES) are expected to support both reliable assessment and actionable instructional feedback. However, existing approaches often treat scoring and feedback as separate components: neural scoring models provide limited interpretability, while Large Language Model (LLM)-based feedback is typically insensitive to learners proficiency levels. To address this fragmentation, this work proposes PsyScore, a psychometrically-aware framework that integrates diagnostic assessment with instructional scaffolding through a shared latent ability representation. PsyScore comprises three key modules: a Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer that incorporates the Graded Partial Credit Model (GPCM) into a neural architecture, enabling the precise estimation of student ability while maintaining psychometric interpretability, a ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator, which conditions multi-agent feedback strategies on the diagnosed ability parameter to adapt instructional focus across different proficiency levels, and a Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy that assesses feedback quality via pairwise preference judgements and student revision simulations. Experiments on the ASAP++ dataset demonstrate that PsyScore achieves competitive scoring performance while providing more pedagogically aligned feedback.

</details>


### [53] [CATCH-ME if you RAG: a dataset of Contextually Annotated multi-Turn Counterspeech against Hate and Misinformation Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20369)
*Helena Bonaldi,Genoveffa Martone,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对仇恨言论与虚假信息重叠的复杂问题，研究者构建了首个大规模、多语言、专家策划的多轮对话数据集，并引入外部事实知识和跨度标注，以提升LLM生成反驳言论的质量与事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论与虚假信息往往相互交织，但现有NLP研究多将其独立对待。同时，现有的零样本LLM生成的反驳言论往往重复且模糊，缺乏高质量的多轮次、多语言数据集来引导模型生成更具说服力和事实依据的响应。

Method: 构建了一个大规模、专家策划的综合多语言数据集。该数据集包含多轮对话，通过锚定经核实的外部知识（事实核查文章和非政府组织报告）提供事实依据，并包含文档级和块级的跨度标注，旨在支持检索增强生成（RAG）系统的应用。

Result: 发布了一个覆盖5种语言、针对7个边缘化群体的多轮对话数据集。该成果实现了反驳言论在事实基础上的增强，并为RAG系统的训练与评估提供了高质量的标注资源。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多语言、多轮次、基于外部知识锚定的高质量数据集，为开发能够同时应对仇恨言论与虚假信息交叉问题的反驳言论模型提供了必要的基础，并增强了生成内容的论据支持与针对性。

Abstract: Online hate speech and misinformation frequently overlap, yet NLP research has mainly treated them in isolation. While LLMs represent a scalable solution for assisting humans in the generation of counterspeech for both threats, zero-shot models frequently generate repetitive and vague responses, underscoring the need for high-quality examples to steer model generation. However, existing counterspeech datasets against the overlap of hate and misinformation are scarce and limited to single-turn English dialogues, while real-life interactions span across multiple turns and languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first large-scale, expert-curated, multilingual dataset of dialogues tackling the intersection of hate and misinformation. To ensure factual grounding, the dialogues are also anchored in verified external knowledge (i.e., fact-checking articles and NGO reports) and include document- and chunk-level span annotations, making it directly applicable for RAG systems. Covering five languages and targeting hate directed at seven marginalized groups, this novel resource enables the training and evaluation of more persuasive, factually grounded counterspeech models.

</details>


### [54] [Your Mouse and Eyes Secretly Leak Your Preference: LLM Alignment using Implicit Feedback from Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20482)
*Haw-Shiuan Chang,Jeffrey Gomez,Mehul Patwari,Aryan Sajith,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入隐式人类反馈（鼠标轨迹与眼动数据），并构建了IFLLM数据集，证实了其在提升大语言模型对齐效果方面远优于传统显式文本标注方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流对齐方法严重依赖昂贵且稀缺的显式人工反馈，且忽视了互联网领域广泛存在的、对提升模型表现至关重要的隐式反馈价值。

Method: 构建了包含1336个多轮对话的IFLLM数据集，收集了人类对LLM响应的鼠标轨迹和眼动数据；开发了一个基于隐式反馈的奖励模型，并应用于DPO（直接偏好优化）进行模型对齐。

Result: 基于隐式反馈的奖励模型将文本奖励模型的准确率从55%提升至64%；在八个LLM模型上应用DPO后，响应质量的相对提升幅度几乎翻了三倍。

Conclusion: 隐式反馈（如鼠标轨迹和眼动数据）在对齐大语言模型方面具有显著价值，能够超越仅依赖文本偏好的传统方法，显著提升模型表现。

Abstract: To align a Large Language Model (LLM), most existing methods collect explicit human feedback and train a reward model to predict the human preference based on the response text. These existing methods have two key limitations. First, the users rarely provide explicit feedback for LLM responses, which makes the high-quality preference annotation expensive to collect. Second, the methods do not leverage implicit human feedback, which has proven vital to the economic moats of Internet giants. To quantify the value of implicit feedback, we build a new dataset called IFLLM, which collects 1336 multi-turn questions from the 59 Mechanical Turk workers, their mouse trajectories, and eye gazing points to the LLMs' responses from their webcams. IFLLM shows that the users have very diverse types of gazing behavior and mouse trajectories. Our reward model based on the implicit user feedback boosts the accuracy of the text-based reward model from 55% to 64% and nearly triples the relative response quality improvements after applying the DPO to eight LLMs, demonstrating the value of implicit feedback in the wild. Our data collection website, dataset, and codes can be found at https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20487)
*Shu Yao,Yuhua Luo,Qian Long,Jingru Fan,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuheng Wang,Lin Wu,Yufan Dang,Huatao Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多设备智能体执行中的故障恢复难题，本文提出了H-RePlan框架，通过将本地策略修正与全局重规划解耦，显著提升了复杂跨设备任务的执行稳健性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多设备智能体在面对运行时故障时缺乏精细化恢复能力，通常只是简单重试或盲目重规划，未能区分哪些错误可以在本地修复，哪些需要全局协调，从而限制了任务的成功率。

Method: 提出H-RePlan框架，通过统一API-CLI-GUI接口实现多设备协同，并引入紧凑的跨层失败抽象（Cross-layer Failure Abstraction），将设备本地的策略级恢复与协调器层面的全局重规划分离开来。此外，构建了注入故障的基准测试HeraBench。

Result: H-RePlan在任务完成率、指令遵循度及完美通过率上显著优于现有基准模型，并在实现稳健执行的同时降低了Token成本。

Conclusion: 领域感知的层级恢复机制对于稳健的多设备智能体执行至关重要，H-RePlan通过区分本地修复与全局重规划，显著提升了任务执行的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.

</details>


### [56] [StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20527)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Timo Cavelius,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Samantha Dalal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出StylisticBias基准，通过控制变量法系统性评估了MLLMs对外貌视觉属性的社会偏见，发现模型对特定视觉线索（如年龄、风格）表现出显著且集中的判断偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大模型（MLLMs）应用日益广泛，但其视觉判定背后的社会偏见机制尚不明确，现有研究因无法剥离个人身份与外貌特征而难以精确衡量。

Method: 构建了一个受控基准测试StylisticBias，通过生成500个基础人脸及每个面部约50个单属性变体（共25K张图像），在保持身份恒定的前提下，独立控制视觉属性变量，并对6种MLLMs在25个社交判断场景下进行评估。

Result: 研究发现年龄和体型对身份层面的判断影响最大，而时尚风格等属性是驱动模型判断偏移的主要因素；约15个核心属性导致了近80%的判断差异，表明偏见具有高度集中性。

Conclusion: StylisticBias揭示了MLLMs的社会偏见并非均匀分布，而是高度集中于少数视觉属性（如年龄、体型、时尚风格）。偏见在与外貌语义相关的判断（如社会经济地位）中尤为强烈，该数据集为评估和减轻MLLMs中的细粒度偏见提供了关键标准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.

</details>

### [57] [MonaVec: A Training-Free Embedded Vector Search Kernel for Edge and Offline AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19458)
*Oğuzhan Yenen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MonaVec 是一个类似于 SQLite 的嵌入式向量搜索内核，通过无需训练的量化算法和确定性设计，实现了在资源受限的边缘与离线场景下，高性能、高重现性且易于部署的向量检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的向量搜索系统大多依赖于服务器架构或海量内存，且通常需要预先训练数据，难以满足边缘计算、无网络连接或离线场景下的紧凑部署与高重现性需求。

Method: 1. 采用无训练、数据无关的随机哈达玛变换（RHDH）将向量分布转为标准正态分布。
2. 利用预计算的 Lloyd-Max 表进行 4 位（bit）量化以压缩空间。
3. 基于 Rust 开发，支持跨架构确定性计算，内置 ChaCha20 随机种子以保证结果的可复现性。
4. 提供从 BruteForce 到 IvfFlat 及 HNSW 的多级索引后端支持。

Result: 在 AG News 数据集测试中，MonaVec 在 27MB 的体积下实现了 0.960 的 Recall@10，性能优于 float32 格式的 FAISS-IVF 和 8 位的 usearch，且具备完全的确定性（byte-identical）。

Conclusion: MonaVec 通过“一次封装、无处运行”的文件格式和确定性计算机制，成功解决了端侧与离线环境下高性能向量搜索的部署难题，是边缘人工智能（Edge AI）领域的重要工具。

Abstract: We present MonaVec, a deterministic, embedded vector-search kernel for edge and offline AI -- settings where server infrastructure, network connectivity, and training data are all unavailable. Existing vector-search systems assume a persistent server, gigabytes of RAM, or a training pass over the corpus; MonaVec instead targets the deployment profile of SQLite: one file, one function call, runs anywhere. Its quantization core is training-free by default and data-oblivious: a Randomized Hadamard Transform (RHDH) conditions any input distribution toward N(0,1), so precomputed Lloyd-Max tables quantize to 4 bits (8x smaller) with no learned codebook and no data pass. The index persists as a single .mvec file whose embedded ChaCha20 rotation seed makes results reproducible across architectures and byte-identical within a build -- a determinism guarantee that parallel-build graph libraries cannot offer.
  On semantic embeddings (AG News, 45K x 1024-dim BGE-M3, cosine), MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce reaches 0.960 Recall@10 in 27 MB -- leading float32 FAISS-IVF and 8-bit usearch on recall -- while trading peak throughput for byte-identical determinism. A single-pass global standardization (fit()) extends the same data-oblivious pipeline to magnitude-sensitive L2 data, and optional IvfFlat and HNSW backends carry it to million-vector corpora.
  MonaVec is implemented in pure Rust with Python bindings and runtime SIMD dispatch (AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/scalar). It targets on-device RAG, offline agents, and embedded retrieval -- the niche SQLite occupies for relational data: one file, one call, runs anywhere.

</details>


### [58] [VCG: A Multimodal Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds under Extreme Cold-Start Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19627)
*Katya Mirylenka,Egor Malykh,Mahdyar Ravanbakhsh,Michael Gygli,Marco-Andrea Buchmann,Andrew Dzhoha,Svitlana Borzenko,Francesca Catino,Mohamed Gaafar,Maarten Versteegh,Thomas Kober,Dario d'Andrea,Ellie Langhans*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CLIP的多模态视频召回系统VCG，解决了电商短视频流的极端冷启动与行为偏差难题，显著提升了视频推荐的完播率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台正转向沉浸式短视频流，但面临内容冷启动（缺乏交互历史）以及视频流特有的位置/时长偏差导致传统协同过滤失效的问题。

Method: 构建了可扩展的多模态检索架构，利用领域自适应的视觉-语言模型（基于CLIP）将用户与视频映射至共享语义空间，并对比了生成式（LLM）与判别式（CLIP）嵌入在检索中的表现。

Result: 实验表明生成式模型在检索任务中会出现嵌入空间崩塌；线上A/B测试显示VCG显著降低了偏差，并实现视频完播率50%的增长。

Conclusion: VCG系统通过多模态语义检索成功解决了短视频电商中的极端冷启动问题和行为偏差，证明了判别式模型（CLIP）在检索任务中优于生成式模型（LLM）。

Abstract: The digital commerce landscape is shifting from static, search-driven catalogs to dynamic, immersive video feeds. This transition introduces an ``extreme cold-start'' problem: unlike traditional items, new short-form videos lack the dense interaction history required for collaborative filtering. Furthermore, immersive feeds introduce strong position and duration biases that distort standard engagement signals. In this paper, we demonstrate the Video Candidate Generation (VCG) system, a scalable multimodal retrieval engine designed to solve these challenges in a large-scale e-commerce environment. By leveraging a domain-adapted vision-language model (based on CLIP), we map users and videos into a shared semantic space, enabling zero-shot retrieval based on visual content rather than behavioral history. We detail the system's architecture and present a rigorous evaluation comparing generative (LLM) vs. discriminative (CLIP) embeddings. Our results show that while generative models excel at attribute prediction, they suffer from embedding space collapse in retrieval tasks. Online A/B testing demonstrates that VCG effectively mitigates engagement biases, yielding a 50\% uplift in deep video completion. To showcase the system's capabilities, we present an interactive demonstration featuring three bi-directional retrieval scenarios: Product-to-Video, Video-to-Product, and Zero-Shot Semantic Search.

</details>


### [59] [Token Factory: Efficiently Integrating Diverse Signals into Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19635)
*Xilun Chen,Shao-Chuan Wang,Baykal Cakici,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Raghu Keshavan,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Xinyang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Token Factory”框架，通过将异构推荐特征转化为高效的“软标记”，解决了大推荐模型（LRM）在处理传统特征时的效率瓶颈，实现了性能与计算资源的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将传统推荐信号直接文本化或使用离散项表示的方法，会导致提示词过长、内存消耗巨大及计算开销高等问题，限制了其在大模型架构中的有效集成。

Method: 提出“Token Factory”框架，将传统的推荐信号转化为大模型可以直接处理的“软标记（soft tokens）”，从而实现异构输入特征的有效压缩和集成，规避了由于直接文本化导致的提示词过长问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，Token Factory 在生产级推荐环境中能够显著提升模型性能，并有效缓解了算力和内存压力。

Conclusion: Token Factory通过将传统推荐信号转化为“软标记（soft tokens）”，有效解决了大推荐模型（LRM）在处理异构特征时的效率、存储及提示词长度问题，为工业级推荐场景提供了一种高效且高性能的新方案。

Abstract: Large Recommendation Models (LRMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in industry-scale recommendation tasks. However, holistically integrating traditional signals into these transformer-based architectures effectively and efficiently remains a major challenge. Conventional approaches that "textualize" these signals directly or create discrete item representations often lead to excessively long prompts, substantial memory footprints, and high computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [60] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade 是一套旨在降低图表问答成本的交互系统，通过智能路由机制决定何时调用昂贵的 VLM，实现了在保持高准确度的同时显著降低系统运维成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对图表问答任务，全面调用 VLM 成本高昂且不必要，因为许多问题仅依赖 OCR 文本和基础语言推理即可解决，旨在平衡系统的准确率与计算成本。

Method: 设计了一个交互式系统：首先利用 OCR 提取图表文本，通过轻量级语言模型（LLM）获取初步答案；随后由训练好的随机森林路由器（Router）根据推理时特征决定是否将任务升级至 VLM（视觉语言模型），并提供可视化决策过程以供人工评估和调整阈值。

Result: 在 ChartQA 数据集测试中，SAFE-Cascade 在降低了 26.9% 的 VLM 调用率和 9.3% 预估成本的情况下，取得了 69.1% 的统一准确度（基准为 67.7%），证明了选择性路由在性能与效率间的平衡能力。

Conclusion: SAFE-Cascade 通过选择性模态路由机制，在保持与全 VLM 基准相当准确度的同时，有效降低了系统的推理成本与冗余调用，为构建透明、可调且具有成本意识的多模态系统提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [61] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对语义缓存系统，作者指出传统 PR-AUC 指标导致了评估与部署的脱节，并提出了 P-CHR 和 CRR 指标来更准确地衡量模型，强调语义缓存的选择应优先考虑模型校准能力而非排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存系统普遍采用 PR-AUC 进行模型评估，但该指标忽略了固定阈值下的可用性，导致模型离线评估表现优异但在实际部署中效果不佳。

Method: 1. 指出传统 PR-AUC 指标的局限性；2. 提出两种新指标：P-CHR（缓存精度-缓存命中率曲线下面积）和 CRR（校准保持率）；3. 对离线与部署质量之间的操作缺口进行分解，分为可恢复的“校准组件”和不可约的“结构组件”。

Result: 研究表明，校准缺口主要受训练目标而非数据规模的影响，且事后校准（post-hoc calibration）只能部分弥合这一差距，单纯依靠排序指标无法优化语义缓存系统的性能。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择本质上是一个校准问题而非排序问题，传统的 PR-AUC 指标无法反映实际部署性能，必须结合 P-CHR 和 CRR 等缓存感知指标进行评估，以缩小离线评估与实际部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [62] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar 是一种可扩展的多模态文档检索框架，通过词法过滤和基于磁盘的按需加载技术，在保持高检索精度的同时大幅解决了多向量表示带来的内存与延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态文档检索方法（如多向量晚交互模型）在处理大规模语料时存在巨大的存储开销，导致内存压力大且检索延迟高，难以在现实场景中部署。

Method: 提出 Stellar 框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 基于词法表示的过滤（LRF）：利用多模态大模型（MLLM）作为稀疏编码器进行高效预过滤；2) 基于磁盘的延迟交互（DLI）：设计了聚类引导的磁盘存储布局，并配合代价模型动态按需加载 Token 嵌入。

Result: 在四个基准数据集及一个新建的大规模数据集上的实验证明，Stellar 在保持检索效果的同时，显著提升了系统的可扩展性，内存和延迟表现优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: Stellar 框架通过 LRF 和 DLI 技术，在不损失检索准确性的前提下，将多模态检索系统的内存开销和查询延迟降低了 1 到 2 个数量级，有效解决了大规模多模态文档检索中的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [63] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文分析了大模型代理在长期交互中上下文管理不当的问题，指出由于依赖简单的“最近原则”导致重要信息丢失，提出代理需要一种能按需实时筛选上下文（内存、对话、工具输出）的新型高效管理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型代理在处理长会话时会面临上下文窗口耗尽的问题；现有的“近因截断”策略是主题盲目的，经常丢弃关键的早期信息并保留冗余的近期信息，导致多轮对话和复杂内存任务中的性能下降。现有检索增强（RAG）和压缩技术无法解决已进入上下文池的数据筛选问题。

Method: 本文批判性地分析了现行上下文管理方法的局限性（即缺乏全局相关性评估、盲目删减），并指出未来需要一种能够将内存条目、对话轮次和工具输出视为统一候选池，并根据当前查询需求进行实时筛选的上下文编排机制。

Result: 指出了现有上下文管理方法的“主题盲目性”和“处理局限性”，论证了当前LLM代理在处理长程记忆和复杂工具交互时，不仅需要外部检索，还需要一个能够对当前上下文中已存在的内容进行动态相关性仲裁的方案。

Conclusion: 传统的基于“近因原则”（recency truncation）的上下文管理机制在处理长会话和工具使用时存在严重的缺陷，无法保证信息的语义相关性。作者旨在通过提出一种能够根据当前查询实时评估并从内存、对话记录和工具输出中进行动态筛选的机制，来解决长上下文管理中的信息丢失问题。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [64] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了品牌在生成式AI搜索引擎（GEO）中的可见度问题，通过分析超10万次AI响应数据，揭示了品牌知名度与AI推荐概率的正相关性，识别了“Best-of”列表等关键内容格式的重要性，并为品牌提供了优化AI搜素可见度的基础建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户从传统搜索转向AI驱动的答案引擎（如ChatGPT, Perplexity等），传统的SEO策略已不再适用。品牌亟需理解如何优化其在生成式引擎中的表现（即GEO），特别是对于非头部品牌（中小型企业、D2C品牌、初创公司）而言，如何在AI回答中获得可见度是当前亟待解决的问题。

Method: 使用Ranqo平台追踪了2026年3月至5月期间，针对100多个品牌的超过10万次提示词（prompt）响应数据。通过量化分析品牌在AI回答中的出现频率、被引用来源的类型、以及内容情感倾向，构建了跨AI引擎的可见度指标。

Result: 1. 品牌可见度存在明显的三级阶梯：全球知名品牌（73%）、中端/区域品牌（44%）、小众品牌（11%）；2. 78%的引用指向企业官网；非企业来源中，YouTube表现最优，其次是Reddit、专业媒体和Wikipedia；3. “Best-of”列表类文章是最高效的内容格式（占引用总数的21%）；4. 品牌情感倾向是极其不稳定的指标，其波动频率是品牌提及率的6.7倍。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了衡量生成式引擎优化（GEO）的首个大规模基线。研究表明，AI品牌可见度是可以测量的，且存在显著的平台差异，并与品牌成熟度高度挂钩（呈现明显的“品牌地位阶梯”）。研究最后提出了七项v1.1协议，旨在探索提升AI搜索可见度的因果路径。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [65] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ScholarQuest，这是一个用于评估学术搜索智能体的大规模分类基准，旨在解决现有测试在复杂、多意图学术查询任务中评估不足的问题，并揭示了当前搜索智能体存在的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试无法系统地评估在真实开放文献环境下具备智能体能力的学术搜索系统。

Method: 构建了名为 ScholarQuest 的大规模、分类引导的基准测试，涵盖 1,000 多个计算机科学主题和四种代表性研究意图；配套提供了可扩展的答案构建方法和共享检索后端 ScholarBase 以确保评估的可重复性。

Result: 测试表明，智能体方法优于传统的单次检索基线，但表现最好的智能体在指标上仅达到 0.314 (Recall@100) 和 0.355 (Recall@All)，性能仍有显著不足。分析还涵盖了搜索效率、意图稳健性及故障案例。

Conclusion: ScholarQuest 揭示了当前基于 LLM 的学术搜索智能体在复杂学术查询任务中表现仍有巨大提升空间，且目前的评估框架为开发更高效、稳健的学术搜索系统提供了关键的基准和多维度分析工具。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [66] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态检索中模型忽视细粒度查询信息（粒度盲点）的问题，提出了 ELVA 框架。通过引入基于规则的强化学习和新基准 MRBench，不仅无需显式标注即可优化排序，还显著提升了模型在复杂多粒度查询场景下的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于对比学习的多模态检索（UMR）方法存在“粒度盲点”问题，即模型忽略了查询中关键的细粒度信息。这是因为传统对比学习将样本简化为二分类（正/负），未能充分利用负样本所携带的不同信息量。

Method: 1. 提出了 ELVA 框架，采用基于规则的强化学习（Rule-based RL）驱动 MLLMs 进行排序优化；2. 将强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）扩展至检索任务，无需显式排序标注即可探索最优排序行为；3. 利用基于规则的奖励函数，同时优化负样本排序并扩大正负样本间的相似度差距；4. 构建了专用于衡量粒度盲点的新基准 MRBench。

Result: ELVA 框架在标准检索基准上取得了 SOTA 结果，并在 MRBench 上实现了 13.1% 的性能提升，证明了该方法对缓解粒度盲点的有效性。

Conclusion: ELVA 框架有效解决了多模态检索中的“粒度盲点（Grain Blindness）”问题，不仅在标准检索基准上达到了 SOTA 水平，且在专为多粒度查询设计的 MRBench 上实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [67] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 G2Rec，一个用于生成式推荐的可扩展框架，通过统一全局图共交互建模与项目语义 Token 化，解决了现有方法在注入复杂语义和用户行为上下文时的性能与扩展性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐在处理复杂用户行为和项目语义上下文时存在局限：图方法扩展性差或仅关注局部信息；现有语义 Token 化方法往往依赖启发式规则，缺乏有效监督，导致表示不准确。

Method: 提出 G2Rec 框架：1. 采用全局图（Holistic Graph）建模用户共交互行为；2. 结合语义 Token 化技术，将图信息与语义注入推荐模型，无需人工标注即可学习用户兴趣原型。

Result: 在工业级生产环境（多个产品页面）的部署和公开数据集上的实验显示，G2Rec 在生成式推荐任务中表现优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: G2Rec 成功实现了在生成式推荐中统一全局图信息建模与语义 Token 化。通过在工业环境中的部署和实验，证明了该框架能够更准确、更全面地建模用户行为上下文，提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [68] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的“计算可识别性”框架，通过有限计算搜索过程来判断估计量的可行性，克服了传统因果识别过度依赖理想化数学条件的问题，能够更有效地解决复杂现实数据中的因果推断需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果识别理论主要基于“理论可识别性”，即在无限数据和理想数学条件下推导因果效应。这种理想化的前提往往难以应对现实应用中出现的有限样本、模糊图形判据、观测与干预混合数据以及反事实估计等复杂问题。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”（computational identifiability）框架。该方法将可识别性定义为一个针对经验估计量的有限计算搜索过程。若在给定误差容忍度、参数先验分布以及特定搜索流程下，该过程能搜索到有效的估计量，则视为满足识别条件。

Result: 通过实验证明，该框架成功实现了在小样本、模糊图形标准、混合数据类型及反事实推断等实际场景下的细粒度识别评估，证实了计算代价与识别可行性之间的内在联系。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种从“理论可识别性”向“计算可识别性”的范式转变。这种以计算为核心的方法能够更好地应对现实场景中的挑战，弥合了理论推导与有限数据分析之间的鸿沟，为因果推断提供了更具实用性的评估框架。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [69] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 UltraQuant，一种针对 AMD GPU 优化的 4-bit KV Cache 量化技术，通过专门的硬件内核和量化策略，有效解决了上下文密集型智能体应用中的延迟与吞吐瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体架构给 GPU 的 KV Cache 带来了巨大压力，现有的 KV Cache 存储方案无法在大规模并发下有效平衡任务质量、缓存驻留率和推理吞吐量。

Method: 1. 基于 TurboQuant 的 4-bit KV Cache 量化（旋转和码本量化）；2. 针对 AMD GPU 优化的解码注意力内核；3. UltraQuant 技术：采用 FP4 KV 张量、FP8 查询向量、UE8M0 组缩放及 CDNA4 本地缩放 MFMA 支持。

Result: 在长上下文多轮智能体任务中，UltraQuant 将缓存压力较大的后期回合的 P50 首字延迟降低了 3.47 倍（平均降低 2.3 倍），并将输出吞吐量提升了 1.63 倍。

Conclusion: UltraQuant 显著提升了多轮智能体任务中的服务性能，证明了在保持精度的同时通过量化 KV Cache 实现高吞吐量和低延迟的可行性。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [70] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Guard 框架，通过多教师蒸馏实现将大模型知识迁移至轻量级模型，解决科学预测中基础模型分布偏移与端侧计算资源受限的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在科学领域应用时面临分布偏移和计算成本过高的问题，难以直接进行零样本部署（Zero-shot deployment）。

Method: 提出 Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard) 框架：1. 上下文路由器（Contextual Router）：根据输入数据统计动态选择最相关的教师模型；2. 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature）：在教师模型置信度与领域事实产生分歧时，自动调节蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象、碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网四个领域中，Guard 在 RMSE 指标上优于固定的多教师蒸馏基准；即使教师模型存在分布偏移，该方法也能纠正偏差，并在 28.5% 的困难实例中超越全局最优模型。

Conclusion: Guard 框架通过动态路由和不确定性门控机制，有效克服了时间序列基础模型在科学领域部署时的分布偏移问题，实现了资源受限场景下的高性能科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [71] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型推理的高能耗与提示词中的冗余社会用语，本文提出了SPSD方法利用端侧小模型进行提示词精简，在保证回答质量的同时显著降低了云端输入Token的成本与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型推理中的“预填充”（prefill）阶段能源成本不断上升；用户在对话中包含大量的社会性修辞及礼貌用语，这些内容对人际交流很重要，但对机器推理而言存在语义冗余（即“社会-语义鸿沟”）。

Method: 提出了SPSD（情感保持语义蒸馏）流水线：利用端侧的小型语言模型（4-bit量化的Gemma-2-2B-Instruct）在本地压缩用户提示词，移除其中的“社会脚手架”（即低信息量的礼貌用语等），随后将精简后的提示词发送至云端大模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行推理，并针对安全关键领域设置了规则过滤。

Result: 测试显示平均每次调用减少约99.9个输入Token；“大模型作为评委”评分显示蒸馏方案在响应质量上与原提示词非劣效；每单次调用估计可降低70-270微瓦时的能耗。

Conclusion: 在端侧进行提示词蒸馏（SPSD）是一种有效的技术，能够在保持云端大模型响应质量的前提下，显著减少输入Token数量，从而降低云端推理的能耗与成本。该方法对于处理具有大量社会语用冗余的对话场景尤为高效。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [72] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了医疗扩散模型在GPU上的性能瓶颈，并通过TF32 Tensor Core加持与内存布局优化，在保持高质量生成的前提下，极大提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗扩散模型（Med-DDPM）虽能生成高保真3D MRI，但由于推理时需要数百次U-Net评估，加之内核行为高度异构，导致GPU资源消耗巨大，限制了其广泛部署。

Method: 使用了跨三代NVIDIA架构的全面性能分析，包括内核级运行时分解、指令组合分析、内存系统利用率、Warp级活动和性能分析器优先级分数估计；在此基础上应用了TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D通道最后（channels-last）布局优化。

Result: 优化后，SM周期减少高达100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率提升至原来的9.98倍，IPC（指令数/时钟周期）提升7%。
