We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [182] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINNs训练中超参数敏感及收敛困难问题，本文提出两阶段进化搜索策略，通过先粗筛后精炼的方法，在计算资源受限的情况下显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs的物理信息损失函数高度非凸且涉及多项结构，导致训练收敛不稳定、易陷入局部最优，且对超参数高度敏感，常规优化方法难以有效搜索参数空间。

Method: 提出了一种结合探索与利用的两阶段进化算法框架：第一阶段采用低保真（短epoch）快速筛选候选超参数；第二阶段对筛选出的最优配置进行标准梯度下降精细化训练。

Result: 在对流方程、Klein-Gordon方程和Helmholtz方程的验证中，该算法相比标准训练方案，在相同计算资源限制下均实现了更低的平均误差。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两阶段进化算法策略能有效应对PINNs在大超参数空间下的优化难题，在有限算力预算下显著提升了求解精度和训练鲁棒性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [183] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASYS是一种结合代理模型与符号进化搜索的智能框架，旨在从偏微分方程（PDE）中自动提取具备数学可解释性的解析结构与近似解，填补了数值模拟与人工数学分析之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的计算方法（如数值模拟）和神经网络无法直接提供数学家所需的解的“数学结构”或可解释性表达，而传统的人工推导费时费力。因此，旨在开发一种能够自动发现、构建PDE可解释分析近似解的框架。

Method: 提出一种名为Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS) 的框架，利用代理模型（Agent）结合PDE理论、问题约束和先验搜索经验，生成可微的符号程序。该方法结合了进化搜索（用于优化数学形式）和基于梯度的优化（用于拟合连续参数），实现了自动化的归纳偏置注入及符号回归。

Result: 在五个涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的课题中，ASYS有效恢复了已知解析解，并为缺乏闭式解的问题（如Allen-Cahn 2D和Keller-Segel模型）构建了物理意义明确、可解释的数学表示。

Conclusion: ASYS为PDE解决方案的研究提供了一种超越传统数值模拟和黑盒神经网络的新范式，能够以自动化方式生成具备可解释性的数学结构，辅助数学家进行深入分析。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [184] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于费舍尔信息矩阵的黎曼锐度（SR），解决了传统锐度度量缺乏参数化不变性的缺陷，并从理论和实验上证明了SGD的泛化能力源于其对黎曼平坦极小值的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习领域普遍认为“平坦极小值（Flat Minima）”有助于泛化，但现有的欧几里得锐度度量（如Hessian矩阵的特征值）缺乏参数化不变性，极易受网络重参数化的影响，从而在理论上站不住脚。本文旨在构建一个具有参数化不变性的锐度度量体系，以严谨地解释SGD的泛化机制。

Method: 1. 基于费舍尔信息矩阵（FIM）定义的统计流形，提出了满足参数化不变性的黎曼锐度（SR）度量标准；2. 将小批量SGD的梯度噪声建模为与FIM成正比的协方差结构；3. 通过随机微分方程（SDE）分析，证明了平稳分布在黎曼平坦极小值点处呈指数级集中；4. 引入PAC-Bayes泛化界，形式化了SR与测试性能之间的联系。

Result: 1. 证明了SR在平滑、保函数重参数化下具有不变性；2. 实验结果表明，SR在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上能可靠预测泛化性能，优于传统的欧几里得锐度；3. 验证了SR随学习率/批大小（η/B）的缩放规律与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入黎曼锐度（Riemannian Sharpness, SR）成功解决了深度学习中关于“平坦极小值与泛化能力”关系的理论争议。研究表明，SGD倾向于收敛到SR较低（即黎曼平坦）的极小值点，且SR具有参数化不变性，能够比欧几里得测度更可靠地预测泛化性能。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [185] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAA通过跨批次操作级证据积累机制，解决了批处理追踪蒸馏中的反馈冲突问题，显著提升性能并降低了约75%的训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理式追踪蒸馏中，同一内存操作在不同批次间可能出现反馈矛盾，现有方法缺乏细粒度的跨批次证据积累机制，无法区分有效操作与随机噪声。

Method: 提出了边缘优势积累（Marginal Advantage Accumulation, MAA）方法。该方法通过构建差异信号实现跨批次可比性，利用指数移动平均（EMA）进行操作级证据积累，并通过语义一致性合并（semantic identity merging）确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准测试和4个目标模型共16个实验设置中，MAA在其中14个设置中达到最优，性能超过现有的批处理基准，且在多数情况下媲美甚至超过在线蒸馏方案，同时优化阶段的token消耗降低了约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了批处理式追踪蒸馏中跨批次证据积累的问题，显著提升了蒸馏效果并大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [186] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“传染网络”框架，量化了大语言模型在多智能体系统中评估偏见的传播现象，证明了偏见会跨智能体扩散，并提出通过扩大评估委员会规模可实现有效 mitigation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为多智能体系统的评估者时，其系统性评估偏见会在智能体网络中传播，目前缺乏对这种偏见传染机制的定量测量和缓解策略。

Method: 引入“传染网络（Contagion Networks）”形式化框架，通过3智能体受控实验，计算跨智能体传染矩阵Gamma_3，研究三种评估偏见配置下的传播机制，并分析谱半径对传播的影响。

Result: 证实了评估偏见在智能体间的传染性（gamma在0.157到0.352之间）；识别出由谱半径控制的三个传播机制；发现同模型智能体间的传染系数远低于异模型间，且增大评估者委员会规模能有效抑制传染。

Conclusion: 评估者偏见在多智能体系统中具有传染性，但可以通过增加评估委员会规模（k=3）来显著降低（72.4%）。同模态模型间的偏见传染弱于异模态模型。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过多任务上下文学习实现摊销贝叶斯推理的新框架，通过将先验信息作为上下文输入，使模型能快速适应不同的先验，在保持高预测精度的同时大幅提升了计算效率和分布外任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摊销贝叶斯推理（如Prior-Data Fitted模型）通常高度依赖训练时的先验分布，导致在面对分布偏移（distribution shift）时鲁棒性较差。研究旨在解决如何构建一种能够泛化到新先验分布且具备高计算效率的贝叶斯预测推理模型。

Method: 引入了一个多任务上下文学习（in-context learning）框架，利用Transformer架构将先验任务和目标任务的数据序列作为输入（prefixing）。通过在多样化的先验分布族上进行训练，使模型学会根据上下文数据动态调整推断过程，从而实现对新任务的自适应。

Result: 在多项难度递增的评测任务中（包括超出分布范围的先验和高维潜变量结构），该模型表现出与Oracle Bayesian预测器相当的性能；推理速度比传统方法快几个数量级，并在时空温度预测的真实场景中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于多任务上下文学习的摊销贝叶斯推理方案。该方法通过将先验信息作为上下文输入，提升了模型对不同任务先验的适应能力，在保持高效推理的同时达到了接近最优贝叶斯预测器的性能，证明了通过学习实现通用贝叶斯推理的可行性。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [188] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“隐私即预测性”的新框架，通过更精确地建模攻击者的先验知识，解决了差分隐私在特定场景下过度保守的问题，为隐私保护与模型精度之间的权衡提供了更具细粒度的处理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）的“最坏情况”保护机制通常导致隐私与数据精度之间存在高昂的权衡成本。现有方法缺乏对特定攻击者先验知识和特定敏感查询的精细化建模，因此需要一种更具针对性的隐私度量框架。

Method: 该研究通过引入“隐私即预测性”（Privacy via Predictability）框架，基于攻击者对数据集已知部分的掌握程度（基于随机过程生成的受损部分）及特定查询家族，量化隐私泄露。同时，利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析了在平稳遍历混合过程下的渐近预测性，并提出了针对经验风险最小化（ERM）的预测性校准输出扰动方案。

Result: 研究表明，“隐私即预测性”与差分隐私在数学上总体不可比。在最坏情况下，具备预测性对互信息差分隐私具有蕴含关系。通过实验分析，该方法能够提供比DP更细致的隐私控制，特别是在特定攻击模型下效果更为显著。

Conclusion: “隐私即预测性”提供了一种针对特定敏感信息和攻击者模型的细粒度隐私度量，是差分隐私的有效补充，并在实践中可用于实现更精准的隐私-精度权衡。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [189] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了DiffusionGemma模型的推理透明度。通过构建可解释的token瓶颈，研究克服了扩散模型在变量透明度上的劣势，分析了其独特的（甚至非线性的）推理机制，并证明其在下游任务的实时监控能力上与传统自回归模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的推理透明度对于理解决策、规避风险和调试行为至关重要。由于DiffusionGemma等扩散模型在连续潜在空间进行大量计算，其透明度受到质疑，本文旨在评估扩散模型相对于传统自回归模型的透明度特征。

Method: 1. 定义透明度为变量透明度（中间状态的可理解性）和算法透明度（重构推理过程的能力）。
2. 在DiffusionGemma中引入可解释的token瓶颈，映射降噪步骤间的信息流动。
3. 通过对比“不透明串行深度”量化变量透明度。
4. 结合一系列可解释性案例研究（Case Studies）探究扩散模型的分布式算法演化。
5. 通过可监控性（Monitorability）实验验证模型在下游任务中的实用性。

Result: 1. 引入token瓶颈后，DiffusionGemma的不透明串行深度从初始的28.6倍降至1.1倍（接近Gemma 4）。
2. 揭示了扩散模型特有的推理现象，如非时间顺序推理、Token/序列平滑现象以及中间上下文推理。
3. 实验证明DiffusionGemma在下游任务中具有与Gemma 4相当的可监控性。

Conclusion: 虽然扩散模型架构（如DiffusionGemma）在本质上具有更高的计算深度，但通过引入可解释的标记瓶颈（token bottleneck），可以有效地降低其“不透明串行深度”，使其可解释性达到与自回归模型（Gemma 4）相当的水平。扩散模型展现出独特的推理特征，且其输出具备与自回归模型同等水平的可监控性。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对传统模型无法建模区块链共识机制的问题，本文提出利用拓扑理论（Topos Theory）作为描述区块链系统局部一致性与全局真值构建的天然数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链形式化模型（如 FSM）将共识机制视为模型外部的实现细节，忽略了共识作为分布式系统中核心计算现象的本质，导致现有模型未能真实反映区块链的运作逻辑。

Method: 通过对比论证，指出传统计算理论（有限状态机 FSM）无法涵盖区块链核心的共识过程，进而提出引入范畴论中的“层论”（Sheaf Theory）及其相关的拓扑理论作为新的形式化建模工具。

Result: 确立了将拓扑理论应用于区块链建模的理论可行性，强调了通过局部一致性视角构建全局状态的能力。

Conclusion: 拓扑理论（Topos Theory）是描述分布式系统（如区块链）中“局部一致性到全局真值构建”的天然数学语言，能够有效弥补传统有限状态机模型在处理共识机制上的不足。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [191] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对传统图模型无法描述现代分布式复杂系统的问题，本文提出使用数学化的“层论（Sheaf Theory）”进行建模，并利用“层拉普拉斯算子”解决分布式网络的数据融合与一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统简单图模型在描述现代分布式系统时存在局限性，无法应对异构代理、高维多模态数据以及上下文相关的复杂交互关系。

Method: 引入层论（Sheaf Theory）作为描述复杂系统关系的新数学框架，并提出利用层拉普拉斯算子实现数据融合与共识机制。

Result: 证明了层论在复杂网络建模中的适用性，并验证了层拉普拉斯算子在解决分布式系统中数据融合与网络一致性问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: 层（Sheaf）理论能够为现代分布式系统提供更强大的表达能力，且层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）是实现分布式感知网络中数据融合与一致性建模的有效机制。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [192] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SAC 是首个针对稀疏注意力模型优化的离散 KV 缓存系统，通过 CXL 总线实现细粒度的按需拉取，解决了传统 RDMA 方案在处理稀疏 KV 缓存时的传输与存储效率低下问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大模型推理受限于内存容量，传统的 RDMA 离散内存方案在面对稀疏注意力模型时，因强制加载全量 KV 缓存而导致严重的传输瓶颈和内存浪费。

Method: 提出 SAC (Sparse Attention Cache) 系统：利用 CXL 总线的低延迟、缓存行（Cache-line）粒度的读写语义，实现仅按需抓取稀疏注意力模型所需的 Top-k KV 缓存条目，而非全量拉取。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 SGLang 框架下的评估结果显示：吞吐量提升 2.1 倍，首字延迟（TTFT）降低 9.7 倍，每令牌时间（TBT）降低 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: 利用 CXL 的细粒度内存访问能力，SAC 在稀疏注意力模型推理中显著优于传统的 RDMA 架构，确立了 CXL 作为稀疏大模型离散内存架构的核心地位。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [193] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) 网络中非互质情况下的边不相交哈密尔顿圈（EDHC）问题，本文提出了一种基于局部开关演算的构造方法。该方法最小化了组件间的切换次数，通过符号化的代数证明取代了计算枚举，为特定参数下的EJ网络提供了一种简洁的公式化分解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非互质Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) 网络EDHC分解方法依赖于复杂的矩形表示和交换计划。作者旨在开发一种更简洁、以公式为驱动且具有更直接的代数特征的构造方法，特别是针对那些可以通过符号化方式证明补集关联的欧几里得余数系列。

Method: 在该论文中，作者摒弃了传统用于处理非互质EJ分解的矩形表示法和交换计划，转而在天然的凯莱几何（Cayley geometry）框架下开发了一套局部开关演算工具。通过引入 $d-1$ 次组件间切换来构建前两个哈密尔顿圈，并利用剩余边构建第三个循环。证明过程分解为四个部分：组件标签塌陷、锚点抵消、提升开关代表元的无碰撞性以及连通补集关联。所有定理通过代数证明，而非数值枚举。

Result: 论文成功实现了对特定参数范围内EJ网络的三条EDHC的公式化构造。通过这种局部开关演算，证明了在无需计算枚举的情况下，可以构造出满足条件的哈密尔顿圈分解，并为相关代数证书提供了明确的范围。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于局部开关演算（local switch calculus）的机制，用于构造Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) 网络中的三条边不相交哈密尔顿圈（EDHC）。该方法通过最小化开关数量并利用符号化的补集关联（complement incidence）证明，提供了一种紧凑的公式化构造方案。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [194] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了有向循环网络中的中继容错性，提出了一套基于循环差分条件的设计框架与算法，证明了合理的生成器选择对于实现高效故障容错至关重要，并优于传统的区间生成器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环互联网络具有对称寻址和紧凑生成器等优势，但在有向循环图中评估和实现故障容错的两个跳跃原语（two-hop primitive）缺乏系统性的度量与设计框架。

Method: 通过形式化循环差分重数条件，构建了一个网络设计框架；引入了 $R(n,m)$（最差情况共享中继重数）和 $D_f(n)$（容错度）参数；开发了中继表预处理与查找算法，并进行了大规模（526,539个生成器集）的实证基准测试。

Result: 研究揭示了度数与冗余度之间的权衡关系；提出的优化设计达到了理论计数下界的 1.16 到 1.63 倍，显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 生成器选择对循环网络的容错性至关重要；优化后的阈值设计能以接近理论下界的开销实现 $f$-中继容错，而传统的区间生成器即使增加度数也可能存在结构性失效缺陷。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [195] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对稠密 EJ 网络提出了一种名为 EJ-MOEM 的广播修复算法，通过定向树剪枝与外部边连接策略，在保证树结构的前提下，实现了单/双节点故障下的最优深度修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决稠密 Eisenstein-Jacobi (EJ) 网络在面对节点故障时，如何实现无冗余、低开销且保持树结构的“一对多”广播修复问题。

Method: 提出了一种多定向边最小修复方法 EJ-MOEM，包含三个关键步骤：评估六边形广播树方向、通过故障感知选择候选架构、利用外部跨组件修复边将健康组件重新连接成支撑树。并利用三带表示、扇区后缀附加引理等几何分析方法证明了其理论边界。

Result: 1. 证明了对于连通的故障修剪分量，使用 $c-1$ 条外部修复边是修复的充要条件；2. 深度证书定理证明了单/双故障下的修复深度上限分别为 $t+1$ 和 $t+2$；3. 通过 $t=2$ 到 $t=200$ 的广泛实验验证，系统修复成功率为 100%。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM 是一种高效的容错广播修复方法，能够在 EJ 网络遭遇故障时保证树结构的重新连通。理论证明与大规模实验验证了其在修复深度上拥有 $t+1$ (单故障) 和 $t+2$ (双故障) 的最优边界。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [196] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究通过对以太坊和Base的实证分析表明，EVM当前的固定Gas计量模型未能反映现实中高度波动的资源消耗和状态依赖性，支持通过多维Gas定价和状态增长显式计费来提升系统可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EVM Gas机制假设资源组合固定且执行条件稳定，但在现实中，状态漂移和资源使用模式的变化导致Gas定价偏离实际计算成本，影响了交易准确性和网络性能。旨在量化这些假设失准的程度，并探索多维Gas定价的必要性。

Method: 针对2025年以太坊（L1）和Base（L2）的链上数据进行追踪级（trace-level）测量研究。每日采样3000个区块，将交易分解为操作码级（opcode-level）执行Gas、内在Gas、退款及持久化状态增量。通过跨时间点的回溯执行（在旧状态上运行2025年9月的交易）来评估状态敏感性及其对Gas使用和存储访问模式的影响。

Result: 1. 资源组合极不稳定：Base存储读取和计算占比高，以太坊则倾向于存储写入；以太坊扩容后计算占比增加。2. 状态敏感性显著：Base上46.0%的交易因状态不同导致Gas开销波动，以太坊为13.9%。3. 状态增长成本被低估：Base在2025年产生的持久化状态增长（456GB）远高于以太坊（38GB），现有定价机制未能反映此长期成本。4. 状态依赖性限制了存取列表的有效性和并行执行效率。

Conclusion: 现有的基于单标量（且假设条件稳定）的EVM Gas模拟方案已不再适用。研究证实了EVM工作负载的高度动态性和状态敏感性，建议必须转向多维Gas计量模型，并对持久化状态增长进行显式定价，以解决交易可预测性和并行执行的复杂性问题。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [197] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ODB是一种无需修改模型或内核的轻量级DataLoader适配器，通过延迟批次构建至预处理后，精确匹配样本计算成本，大幅提升了多模态/LLM训练过程中的吞吐量，同时保证了分布式训练的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练中，样本的真实计算成本（受填充、内存占用、GPU利用率影响）只能在复杂的预处理流水线后确定。传统的离线批处理（Offline Batch Samplers）因无法观测到这些动态变量，导致GPU利用率低下且无法充分利用算力。

Method: 提出在线动态批处理（Online Dynamic Batching, ODB）系统。该系统作为DataLoader的插件，将批次形成逻辑延后至数据预处理、增强、分词及多模态标记化之后，并形式化定义“分布式组对齐问题（DGAP）”以确保死锁抑制与对齐。

Result: ODB在多种模型（Qwen3-VL等）和数据集（UltraChat, LLaVA等）上表现优异，相比标准固定批处理，吞吐量提升1.58-4.43倍；相比离线token预算预测器（GMT/BMT），在保持模型质量的前提下，性能更胜一筹。

Conclusion: ODB通过将批次构建推迟到处理流水线的末端，实现了对样本实际计算成本的精确感知。它解决了分布式训练中批次对齐的难题，无需预计算长度缓存或修改模型架构，即可在高异构性的LLM微调任务中显著提升吞吐量。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [198] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一款专为超大规模 GPU 集群设计的低开销、全时段细粒度分布式训练性能监控与诊断系统，通过极高比例的数据压缩和分层分析技术，在低于 2% 的资源占用下实现了性能异常的自动感知与根因定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM训练监控存在冲突：粗粒度指标无法定位根因，而传统细粒度分析工具开销过大（5%-30%）且产生海量数据，无法在生产环境中全时部署。

Method: 提出了一种分层观测架构，将开销降低至2%以下；通过构建统一数据流水线，将原始内核事件压缩约3,700倍；开发了级联式（逐级深入的）诊断框架，通过迭代级、阶段级和内核级分析自动定位故障。

Result: 在10,000+ GPU集群中稳定运行超过6个月；将每Rank每步的数据量从10 MB压缩至2.7 KB；成功识别了包括链接降级、FlashAttention JIT停顿及计算拖后腿等复杂性能异常。

Conclusion: ARGUS 证明了在超大规模（10,000+ GPU）集群中实现低开销、细粒度的全时监控是可行的，能够有效解决复杂的训练性能诊断问题，并显著提升运维效率。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [The Representational Limit of Scalar Interactions: An Interventional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19410)
*Potito Aghilar,Sabino Roccotelli,Stanislao Fidanza,Vito Walter Anelli,Sebastiano Stramaglia,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Stochastic Hi-Fi 是一种新型因果特征交互分解方法，通过去混淆唯一性、冗余性和协同性，精准量化特征交互，显著提升了可解释性推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号化两两交互得分广泛存在将唯一性（U）、冗余性（R）和协同性（S）混淆的问题，导致无法精确揭示特征间的交互机制，特别是在多变量交互（如 XOR）场景下效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种名为 “Stochastic Hi-Fi” 的事后（post-hoc）、无需重训练的可解释性分解方法。该方法基于干预性掩码推理（interventional masked inference），结合了耦合钻石采样（coupled diamond sampling）以减少方差，并提供了有限样本的蒙特卡罗界限及收敛性保证。

Result: 在表格型结构因果模型（SCM）中，该方法恢复交互幅度的比例最高提升了 411 倍；在 GPT-2 的 IOI 电路中成功分离出冗余和协同机制；在 NIH ChestX-ray14 数据集上，该方法在 Deletion AUC 指标上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Stochastic Hi-Fi 能够有效分离交互作用中的唯一性（U）、冗余性（R）和协同性（S），在合成数据与实际深度学习模型（如 GPT-2、ChestX-ray14）的复杂特征分析中表现优异，优于现有的标量交互基准。

Abstract: Signed pairwise interaction scores fundamentally conflate uniqueness (U), redundancy (R), and synergy (S). We prove this on a minimal 3-way XOR structural causal model: faithful indices such as Shapley-Taylor return zero per pair, whereas projective indices such as Shapley Interaction spread the third-order effect into pair scalars that conflate the three mechanisms. We introduce Stochastic Hi-Fi, a post-hoc, retraining-free predictability decomposition that estimates per-feature U/R/S profiles by interventional masked inference. The estimator provides exact interventional semantics, finite-sample Monte Carlo bounds, strict variance reduction from coupled diamond sampling, and uniform finite-vocabulary convergence. Across tabular SCMs, Stochastic Hi-Fi recovers structure missed by scalar baselines (up to 411x larger interaction-magnitude recovery ratios). It also separates redundant and synergistic heads in the GPT-2 IOI circuit. On NIH ChestX-ray14, Stochastic Hi-Fi matches GradCAM on Pointing Game and improves substantially on Deletion AUC.

</details>


### [200] [A Solver-Free Training Method for Predict-then-Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19587)
*Beichen Wan,Mo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对预测后优化任务，本文提出了一种无需优化求解器的决策导向学习算法，通过测度变换解决梯度难计算与计算成本高的问题，在保证决策质量的前提下极大提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在“预测后优化（predict-then-optimize）”框架下，直接最小化经验决策遗憾因决策映射呈分段常数特征导致梯度几乎处处为零而难以求解；现有通过平滑处理实现梯度计算的方法通常需要在每次梯度下降中调用代价高昂的优化求解器，导致扩展性差（计算开销大）。

Method: 提出了一种基于测度变换（measure transformation）原理的决策导向学习流水线，构建了一个完全无需优化求解器的替代损失函数（surrogate loss）来替代直接最小化难以求导的经验决策遗憾。

Result: 理论上：证明了该方法的费雪一致性（Fisher consistency）和超额风险界（excess risk bounds）；实验上：该方法在决策质量上与现有最先进方法持平，且训练时间缩短了几个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种名为“基于测度变换（measure transformation）”的决策导向学习新方法，通过完全免除训练过程中的优化求解器调用，在保持与现有领先方法具备竞争力的决策质量的同时，实现了训练效率的大幅提升（几个数量级）。

Abstract: We propose a scalable method for training prediction (machine learning) models in the predict-then-optimize paradigm, where model outputs serve as coefficients for a subsequent linear optimization task. Directly minimizing the empirical decision regret is intractable for linear programming and combinatorial optimization since the decision mapping is piecewise constant, and the gradients are zero almost everywhere. While existing methods address this by smoothing the differentiation process, they suffer from scalability issues, since a computationally expensive solver call is required for every gradient evaluation. To address this, we propose a decision-focused learning pipeline based on a measure transformation principle, which yields a new surrogate loss that is completely optimization-solver-free during training. We establish theoretical guarantees, including Fisher consistency and excess risk bounds. Empirically, our method achieves decision quality competitive with state-of-the-art methods while reducing training time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [201] [Variational Consensus Monte Carlo for Bayesian Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19643)
*Julie Fendler,Francesca L. Crowe,Tom Marshall,Sylvia Richardson,Paul D. W. Kirk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在隐私保护联邦学习环境下推断贝叶斯混合模型的方法，通过改进变分共识蒙特卡洛算法，实现了无需数据汇总即可处理过拟合混合模型、自动确定聚类数量，并成功应用于大规模电子病历数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗健康数据具有高度敏感性和隐私性，难以进行中心化共享或合并，这限制了现有贝叶斯混合模型在医疗领域的大规模应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于变分共识蒙特卡洛（Variational CMC）的联邦学习管线：(1) 将变分CMC扩展至过拟合贝叶斯混合模型，支持自动确定聚类数量且无需共轭性；(2) 设计了跨节点的聚类匹配算法，解决局部数据集缺失某些聚类的问题；(3) 针对不同的隐私/联邦约束提出了多种聚合策略。

Result: 通过模拟实验验证了框架的有效性，并与现有联邦学习方法进行了比较。结果表明，当局部数据反映潜在聚类结构时，该方法在恢复小规模聚类方面比集中式MCMC更准确，并成功应用于大型电子病历数据中识别老年病多发病率模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种适用于过拟合贝叶斯混合模型的联邦学习框架，证明了其在无需合并数据的情况下，能够有效推断数据聚类结构，甚至在捕捉小规模聚类方面表现优于集中式MCMC。该研究为保护隐私的医疗多发病率数据分析提供了可行的落地路径。

Abstract: Motivated by the privacy, sensitivity and sharing limitations of health data, we present a comprehensive pipeline for inference of Bayesian mixture models within a federated learning setting, i.e. when data cannot be fully shared or pooled across compute nodes. We adopt a Consensus Monte Carlo (CMC) approach, in which an MCMC algorithm is run independently within each data silo to estimate local posterior distributions, which are then aggregated to approximate the posterior over the full data. The variational CMC approach of Rabinovich, Angelino and Jordan (2015) [1] frames the aggregation step as a variational inference problem, but their application to mixtures assumes the number of clusters and key mixture parameters to be known. Our main methodological contributions are: (i) an extension of variational CMC to over-fitted Bayesian mixture models that infer the number of clusters and all model parameters, without requiring conjugacy; (ii) novel cluster-matching algorithms suitable for cross-silo settings in which not every cluster appears in each local dataset; (iii) a number of inference strategies for the aggregation step, matched to different federated learning constraints; and (iv) guidelines for choosing among these in practice. A comprehensive simulation study validates the framework and allows us to compare to state-of-the-art federated learning alternatives. Notably, we show that when the composition of local datasets reflects the underlying clustering structure in the data, our approach can recover small clusters with greater accuracy than standard MCMC applied to the pooled data. We illustrate the framework on large-scale electronic health record data, identifying multi-morbidity patterns in a British geriatric population.

</details>


### [202] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement for LLM-as-a-Judge Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19714)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Weiyi He,Junxi Zhang,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对LLM作为裁判（LLM-as-a-judge）时评估不准且缺乏标注数据的问题，AURA框架通过迭代学习人类一致性并智能引导人工审核，实现了无需大规模预标注的高质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLM）评估框架通常依赖于已标注的可靠样本或人工监督，但在实际应用中，这种假设往往难以成立，且容易继承裁判本身的偏差，导致评估结果与人类评价不一致。

Method: 提出了AURA（Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement）框架，将对LLM裁判的信任度视为一种潜变量，通过迭代学习人类一致性信号、传播可靠证据，并利用不确定性采样机制筛选出最需要人工介入的样本进行验证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实LLM生成的成对数据上经过充分测试，验证了AURA能够通过少量人工干预有效修正LLM的偏差，构建出更符合人类偏好的评估系统。

Conclusion: AURA框架通过结合不确定性感知与迭代人类反馈，为LLM作为裁判的性能评估提供了一个稳健、可扩展的方案，证明了在缺乏高质量标注数据的情况下，通过主动学习和信任建模可以有效提升自动化评估的精确度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for open-ended generation, as large-scale human evaluation is often expensive and difficult to scale, yet their preferences remain imperfect proxies for human judgment. Existing auditing pipelines often assume that a reliable subset of examples or clean supervision signals are available beforehand, for example from human annotation, heuristic filtering, or the outputs of strong judges. In LLM evaluation, this assumption is fragile: the initial split may inherit judge bias, while human verification is typically too scarce to define stable groups at scale. We propose AURA, an adaptive uncertainty--aware refinement framework for auditing pairwise LLM--as--a--judge decisions under selected human verification. AURA iteratively learns a human-consistency signal, propagates reliable evidence, and prioritizes uncertain comparisons for human review. The key idea is to treat trust in a judge as a latent quantity that is progressively refined as evidence accumulates. We provide a compact formulation, a stable refinement procedure, and a comprehensive evaluation on both synthetic and real pairwise LLM-answer data.

</details>


### [203] [Stochastic Linear Contextual Bandits with Bounded Noise: A Set-Membership Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20022)
*Haonan Xu,Yingying Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对随机线性上下文赌博机问题，利用有界奖励噪声的强假设，通过集成员估计（SME）与乐观原则（OFU）提出了SME-OFU算法，实现了从 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 到 $O(\log T)$ 的显著懊悔界改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机线性上下文赌博机（SLCB）研究多假设奖励噪声服从次高斯分布，导致最优懊悔界为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$。而在许多实际应用中，观测到的奖励是严格有界的，这一更有价值的信息尚未被现有文献充分利用。

Method: 引入了集成员估计（Set-Membership Estimation, SME）方法来进行不确定性量化，并结合“面对不确定性保持乐观”（Optimism in the Face of Uncertainty, OFU）原则设计了SME-OFU算法。该方法显式利用了观察到的奖励是有界的这一先验信息。

Result: SME-OFU算法实现了 $O(\log T)$ 的懊悔界，理论上优于次高斯噪声假设下的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 界。仿真实验验证了在有界噪声环境下，该算法相比现有基准算法表现出更好的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 在有界奖励噪声的约束下，相比于传统的次高斯噪声假设，所提出的SME-OFU算法能够实现对数级的懊悔（regret）界，即O(log T)。这一结果证明了利用有界噪声的附加信息可以显著提升线性上下文赌博机的收敛速度。

Abstract: This paper considers stochastic linear contextual bandits (SLCB) with bounded reward noise. Existing works typically assume sub-Gaussian reward noise and bounded expected rewards, under which the optimal regret bound scales as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ in terms of horizon $T$. However, in many applications, realized/observed rewards are also naturally bounded, implying bounded reward noise. Bounded noise is more informative than the sub-Gaussian condition but has not been leveraged explicitly in the SLCB literature. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm SME-OFU by utilizing an uncertainty quantification method called set-membership estimation (SME) and applying the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty (OFU). Our algorithm enjoys an improved regret bound $O(\log T)$. Notice that this does not contradict the existing optimal bound $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ for sub-Gaussian noise because bounded noise is a stronger condition. Finally, simulations show empirical improvements of SME-OFU over a benchmark algorithm designed for sub-Gaussian noise when the reward noise is bounded.

</details>


### [204] [AK-MCS-C2 : Active Kriging Monte Carlo Simulation method with conformal certification for failure probability estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20191)
*Edgar Jaber,Vincent Chabridon,Mathilde Mougeot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合主动克里金算法与一致性预测的结构可靠性分析框架，通过自适应交叉一致性策略，有效提升了小样本下失效概率估计的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟 (AK-MCS) 方法在处理小样本数据时，难以提供有效的预测误差界限，导致在失效极限状态面附近的样本分类存在不确定性，进而影响稀有事件失效概率估计的精度。

Method: 提出了一种集成“主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟 (AK-MCS)”与“一致性预测 (Conformal Prediction)”的框架；引入了基于 J+GP 一致性估计器的自适应交叉一致性 (Adaptive Cross-Conformal) 策略。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法相比经典方法提供了更可靠的分布无关误差保证，在分类精度和失效概率估计的稳健性方面表现更优，尤其适用于稀有事件区间。

Conclusion: 该研究通过集成自适应交叉一致性预测框架，解决了传统AKI-MCS在小样本下不确定性量化不足的问题，为结构可靠性分析中稀有事件的失效概率估计提供了更稳健、具备分布无关保证的方法。

Abstract: We introduce a novel active-learning framework for failure probability estimation in structural reliability analysis that integrates Active Kriging Monte Carlo simulation with conformal prediction. The proposed approach employs an adaptive cross-conformal strategy specifically designed for small-sample settings and kriging surrogate models using the J+GP conformal estimator. Unlike standard AK-MCS methods, the proposed framework provides distribution-free guarantees on prediction errors, leading to more reliable classification of samples near the limit-state surface. This improved uncertainty quantification enhances both the accuracy and robustness of failure probability estimates, especially for rare-event regimes where such efficiency is crucial. Reproducible numerical results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and also compare it to classical approaches on well-established benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Missingness-Aware Policies in MDPs with Rewards Missing Not at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20206)
*Ziheng Wei,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对离线强化学习中奖励非随机缺失（MNAR）导致的选择偏差问题，本文提出了一种基于桥接函数和影子变量的稳健离线策略评估（OPE）框架，有效提升了在现实不完整数据环境下的评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线强化学习中，由于记录稀疏、不规则或奖励值截断，导致奖励数据呈现“非随机缺失（MNAR）”特征，这破坏了可忽略性假设并引入了选择偏差，阻碍了准确的离线策略评估。

Method: 提出了一个依赖于奖励的倾向性模型，利用未来状态作为影子变量（Shadow Variables）来识别全数据条件平均奖励；引入了一种通过最小-最大（min-max）程序估计的桥接函数（Bridge Function），避免了显式建模MNAR机制及双重采样问题；构建了基于拟合Q评估（Fitted-Q-Evaluation）的离线策略评估估计器。

Result: 理论上证明了估计器的一致性和有限样本误差界；在模拟环境和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在处理奖励缺失问题时显著优于现有主流评估方法。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的方法能够有效缓解离线强化学习中非随机缺失（MNAR）奖励带来的选择偏差，为处理实际应用中（如医疗和营销）不完整奖励数据提供了可靠的离线策略评估框架。

Abstract: In offline Reinforcement Learning, immediate rewards in logged batch data are often unobserved due to sparse or irregular record-keeping, or censored beyond certain reward values. This issue arises in practical settings, including health care and marketing. We investigate off-policy evaluation (OPE) in finite-horizon Markov decision processes when rewards are missing not at random (MNAR), which breaks ignorability and induces selection bias even after conditioning on states and actions. To address this, we formalize a reward-dependent propensity model and use future states as shadow variables to identify the full-data conditional mean reward. We further introduce a bridge function that recovers the conditional mean reward without explicitly modeling the MNAR mechanism, and estimate it via a min-max procedure to avoid double sampling. Building upon these identification results, we propose an Fitted-Q-Evaluation-style estimator that propagates the recovered rewards while allowing target policies to depend on past missingness indicators. Finally, we establish consistency and finite-sample error bounds for our OPE estimator, and show through experiments the strong performance of our method compared to existing methods on simulated and MIMIC-III Sepsis data.

</details>


### [206] [Statistical Properties of Training & Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20299)
*Itay Lavie,Noam Levi,Yonatan Kahn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从物理学视角探讨深度学习，通过分析神经缩放定律及其与物理归纳偏置、约束的关系，揭示了深度学习违背传统统计直觉的内在机制与模型构建逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在实际任务中展现出超越传统统计学的性能，但其内在机理尚不明确。研究者旨在通过物理学的分析框架，揭示深度学习关键特征背后的本质及“反直觉”现象，以填补深度学习理论构建中的认知间隙。

Method: 从物理学（Physics-informed）的视角进行理论回顾与分析，重点探讨深度学习模型构建中的核心选择、神经缩放定律（Neural Scaling Laws）以及物理问题中应用机器学习时的归纳偏置与约束。

Result: 论文明确了神经缩放定律是深度学习的底层规律，同时指出在物理任务中配置归纳偏置和处理约束对于模型性能至关重要，这些因素能够与深度学习的通用缩放行为产生复杂的相互作用。

Conclusion: 深度学习表现出独特的物理性质，其性能遵循神经缩放定律，这些定律受到特定归纳偏置和物理约束的影响。未来的研究应进一步统一统计学习理论与物理学视角，以更好地理解深度学习的涌现行为。

Abstract: Deep learning has managed to evade numerous intuitions from classical statistics to achieve unprecedented performance on a number of real-world tasks. In this article, we investigate the key features and surprises of deep learning from a physics-informed perspective, taking care to point out and justify where possible the many choices inherent in constructing a deep learning model. In particular, we review the phenomenon of neural scaling laws and discuss their interplay with the constraints and inductive biases which may be present when applying machine learning to problems in physics.

</details>


### [207] [SSH-Net: A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Failure Time Distribution Functions under Competing Risks with Application to GPU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20451)
*Jie Min,Yueyao Wang,Mengkun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对工程领域复杂分层数据下的竞争风险建模难题，本文提出了 SSH-Net，通过构建与数据结构关联的子网络架构，显著提升了失效时间预测的准确性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在处理复杂工程场景下具有多层结构的数据时，难以有效捕捉关键信息；此外，复杂的神经网络结构增加了超参数调优的难度。

Method: 提出了一种结构化分段风险深度神经网络（SSH-Net），将神经网络结构与数据的分层结构对齐，利用不同的子网络处理不同类型的协变量，并结合特定原因的竞争风险模型以及惩罚对数似然损失函数进行训练。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟和泰坦GPU失效时间数据验证，SSH-Net 在 Brier score、AUC 和 RMSE 指标上均表现出良好的预测准确性，有效捕捉了失效时间分布函数。

Conclusion: SSH-Net 有效解决了复杂分层结构下竞争风险数据的失效时间预测问题，通过结构化设计提升了模型的可解释性与预测准确性。

Abstract: Competing risks are commonly observed in engineering fields and can bring challenges to time-to-event data modeling when the application scenarios are complicated. Recently, deep neural networks have received great attention for prediction with competing risks, due to their flexibility and high learning capability. However, the complexity of neural network structure brings extra difficulty in hyperparameter tuning based on different data inputs. Additionally, when an engineered system has complex physical structures with multiple hierarchical levels, treating all structural levels as a single group of inputs may fail to capture critical information. To address the issues, we propose a Structured Segmented Hazard Deep Neural Network (SSH-Net) for failure time prediction under cause-specific competing risks framework. Our approach associates neural network structure with data structures, and allows different covariate groups to impact the failure prediction through separate sub-networks. The neural network is constructed based on a cause-specific competing risks model. The SSH-Net outputs cause-specific hazard functions, and utilizes the penalized log-likelihood as the loss function. The prediction accuracy of SSH-Net is validated through simulation studies by evaluating the Brier score, the area under receiver operating characteristic curves (AUC), and the root mean square error (RMSE) of the predicted cause-specific cumulative incident function. We further demonstrate the model's ability to predict failure time distribution functions using the Titan GPU failure time data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体治理挑战，本文提出 AgenticRei 框架，通过引入义务逻辑和本体推理，实现了超越传统“允许/禁止”模型的复杂企业级策略治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由 LLM 驱动的智能体具备高度操作权限，现有的访问控制引擎（如 XACML, Rego, Cedar）仅能处理“允许/禁止”逻辑，无法满足复杂的企业治理需求，如义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决以及特定领域的本体推理。

Method: 提出 AgenticRei 框架；基于 Rei 框架构建义务策略语言（使用 OWL 描述）；通过独立于 LLM 的高性能逻辑引擎在运行时进行策略评估；统一管理智能体工具调用及智能体间通信。

Result: 通过示例证明，AgenticRei 能够表达当前主流生产策略引擎无法处理的安全和隐私治理约束，并能自然地与 A2AS 等工业框架集成。

Conclusion: AgenticRei 利用基于义务（Deontic）的策略语言和高性能逻辑引擎，有效地填补了当前 LLM 智能体在复杂企业治理（包括义务、豁免和冲突处理）方面的空白，且具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [209] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过一种基于语义检索与人工审核的“人在回路”管道，量化了某本科CS课程对CS2013/CS2023教学标准的覆盖与演进差异，发现课程知识覆盖度稳定，但因新标准期望提高导致认知深度达成率有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前本科计算机科学课程缺乏一种可靠且可重复的手段，以量化课程对快速演进的国际教学准则的覆盖程度及结构性差异。

Method: 构建了一个“人在回路”(human-in-the-loop)的评估管道，将计算机科学课程与CS2013/CS2023准则表示为结构化语料库。利用语义检索生成候选匹配，并通过明确的覆盖定义进行人工验证。对比了七种不同的检索器（发现互惠倒数融合算法表现最优），并对比了小型句子模型与长上下文模型的效果。

Result: 1. 该程序对CS2023和CS2013的知识单元覆盖率分别约为49.7%和50.9%，呈现十年稳定状态；2. 约88%的覆盖单元拥有明确的能力表达；3. 达到推荐认知深度的比例由CS2013的95%下降至CS2023的76%，归因于新标准增加了教学期望；4. 识别出了如分布式计算、编程语言基础等全行业普遍存在的结构性盲点。

Conclusion: 该管道提供了一种可复用的方法来量化学术课程与国际教学准则之间的对应关系，揭示了课程覆盖面、能力要求与指导标准演进之间的复杂关系。结果显示，该课程在十年间的知识单元覆盖率保持稳定，但随着CS2023标准的更新，课程在达到推荐认知深度方面正面临新标准带来的挑战。研究强调了在评估中进行语境化检索选择的重要性。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [210] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对当前流行的扩散语言模型（DLMs）进行了首个系统性的对比实验，揭示了其在复杂任务下的性能表现、计算效率及多种推理配置对输出质量的影响，旨在解决该领域缺乏统一评估标准的现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型（DLM）评估标准不统一，且各模型的架构、数据集和推理配置差异巨大，导致难以直观比较其性能与权衡，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 系统性实验分析：选取8个SOTA DLMs，涵盖推理、编码、翻译等8个基准，并在相同训练条件下进行受控实验，同时探究了去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小和并行去掩码策略对模型表现的影响。

Result: 研究证实了DLM的行为受到生成阶段设计选择的强烈制约，揭示了性能提升与计算开销之间的复杂权衡关系，并明确了各模型在不同任务类型下的优缺点。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）在文本生成上展示了独特的计算与性能权衡。该研究强调了部署时的设计选择（如去噪步数、推理策略等）对生成质量与效率的决定性影响，为DLMs的实际应用提供了关键指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [211] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出将LLM多智能体审议建模为受内部“锚点”驱动的动态闭环系统，成功解释了LLM为何能通过多轮交流突破初始群体信念限制，并提供了量化评估该过程有效性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM审议通常模仿人类决策过程，但缺乏对“为何通过多轮交流能提升推理与准确度”的深层机制建模。经典意见动力学（如DeGroot模型）无法解释LLM审议中出现的“自信度超越初始群体范围”的特殊现象。

Method: 将多智能体LLM审议建模为闭环动力学系统，引入“锚点”概念（即独立于邻居影响的内部隐含信念）。通过分析审议过程，从数据中反推该锚点，并验证其对未见审议路径的预测能力（泛化性）。在三个开放权重模型家族上进行了实证分析。

Result: 研究发现，LLM审议中确实存在“锚点”支撑。实验表明，并非所有审议都是简单的加权平均，当锚点远离初始意见时，模型能够突破初始信念空间的限制。这种效应在不同模型族中表现为谱系分布，即锚点的定位决定了其对审议结果的影响强度及能否“跳出”初始建议的边界。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM审议并非简单的意见趋同，而是受限于模型的“锚点”（hidden internal belief）。当锚点远离初始意见时，审议能够突破初始信念的凸包（convex hull），实现性能提升。该研究提出的闭环动力学模型通过恢复锚点，不仅能解释这一现象，还能预测模型在不同审议任务中的表现。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [212] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeXposure-Claw 是一个针对 DeFi 监管设计的金融智能体框架，利用图预测模型与确定性规则约束大模型，解决了 LLM 易产生金融误报的痛点，并提供了监管对齐的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大模型（LLM）难以应对去中心化金融（DeFi）中快速演变的联网信用风险，主要表现为过度解读弱信号导致的高风险错误干预，且目前缺乏监管对齐的评估手段来衡量误报问题。

Method: 提出了 DeXposure-Claw 系统，核心包含三部分：(1) DeXposure-FM（图时间序列基础模型）用于预测暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控器与压力测试场景；(3) 数据健康与置信度门控。同时开发了 DeXposure-Bench 评估基准，引入监管对齐的绝对损失度量和显式误干预率指标。

Result: 基于五年真实周度数据的实验表明，该系统在保证可问责性的同时，显著优化了监管决策的准确性，有效缓解了通用大模型在金融监管任务中的缺陷。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw 系统通过将大模型与图时间序列预测及确定性监控机制结合，证明了在监管场景下比通用大模型更可靠、更具规制导向的执行能力，有效解决了过拟合弱证据及高风险误报问题。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [213] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM处理结构化数据时缺乏真正的“自知之明”，其自信度往往是虚假的。通过引入基于归因差异的跨模型校准和外部归因证据（SHAP），可显著提升其在临床任务中的鲁棒性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理结构化临床数据时，是否具备识别自身知识边界（认识论不确定性）的能力，并寻求减少这种不确定性的方法。

Method: 通过跨模型归因差异（Attribution Divergence）分析，对比Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在预测任务上的行为；引入SHAP特征归因作为辅助证据；构建基于归因差异信号的跨模型校准器（Cross-model Calibrator）进行可靠性评估。

Result: 1. LLM verbalized confidence（口头自信度）是无效的，与准确率关联度低；2. 出现“反向难度效应”；3. Few-shot与SHAP证据结合能显著提升准确率至75.3%；4. 跨模型校准器将预期校准误差（ECE）从0.254降至0.080，有效实现了对模型预测可靠性的评估。

Conclusion: LLM对结构化临床数据的“自信度”是空洞的（无法反映实际准确率），存在“反向难度效应”。通过跨模型归因差异分析，结合少样本提示和SHAP特征证据，可以在无需微调的情况下显著提升性能，并利用跨模型校准器实现准确的可靠性评估，从而解决LLM在结构化数据任务中的“冷启动”认识论问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [214] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续表型结构的对比学习框架，摒弃了传统的离散分组策略，通过可微分的加权函数建模受试者间的连续相似性，实现了更精准的阿尔茨海默病风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视网膜与临床风险叙事对齐的框架（如REVEAL）多采用离散的表型分组（hard group assignments）进行对比学习，这种硬性分组限制了监督信息的灵活性，且将分组形成与表示学习解耦，难以捕捉表型风险的谱系特征。

Method: 提出了连续化表型结构以优化对比学习：(1) 将受试者间的相似性建模为由视网膜图像和风险概况的模态内嵌入相似性推导出的可微分加权函数；(2) 使用连续聚合算子定义软多正样本关系（soft multi-positive relationships）；(3) 引入软目标对比目标函数（soft-target contrastive objective），实现跨模态对齐与表型结构学习的端到端联合训练。

Result: 在英国生物银行（UK Biobank）的阿尔茨海默病（AD）发病预测任务中，该框架在性能上持续优于基于离散分组的对比学习方法及标准的视觉-语言基准模型。

Conclusion: 提出了一种连续表型结构的建模方法，将表型相似性视为可学习的连续信号而非固定的分组规则。这种方法在处理阿尔茨海默病（AD）风险预测任务时，比离散的分组对比学习表现更优，为大规模多模态神经退行性风险建模提供了鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond Accuracy: Measuring Logical Compliance of Predictive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20208)
*Guillaume Olivier Delplanque,Pierre Genevès,Nabil Layaïda,Zephirin Faure*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了规则违规评分 (RVS)，这是一种与预测精度无关的、用于评估机器学习模型对逻辑规则遵循程度的标准化指标，旨在补齐现有评估体系在逻辑一致性方面的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习评估指标（如预测误差、准确率等）主要关注预测性能，忽略了模型输出是否符合专业领域内的逻辑一致性和约束条件，这在高风险应用中存在隐患。

Method: 提出了一种名为规则违规评分 (RVS) 的评估指标，用于量化模型在预定义逻辑规则（包括硬规则和软规则）下的合规程度，并开发了基于 SQL 查询的自动化计算方法，支持多种预测模型和关系数据集。

Result: 在知识图谱链接预测和关系回归等三个基准测试中，RVS 成功突显了不同模型在逻辑合规性上的本质差异，揭示了传统指标无法捕获的模型行为信息。此外，RVS 也可用于分析训练数据的逻辑一致性及评估规则定义的质量。

Conclusion: 规则违规评分 (RVS) 提供了一个量化评估模型逻辑一致性的标准化指标。研究表明，仅依靠预测性能指标不足以评估模型质量，RVS 能揭示预测准确率相近模型在逻辑合规性上的显著差异，是现有预测评估框架的重要补充。

Abstract: Machine learning models are predominantly evaluated through predictive performance metrics such as ranking quality, prediction error, or classification accuracy. While these metrics effectively quantify how closely predictions match the ground truth, they do not assess whether model outputs respect predefined logical or domain-specific constraints. In high-stakes applications, including healthcare, finance, and autonomous systems, logical consistency can be as critical as predictive accuracy, yet no standard metric captures this dimension. We introduce the Rule Violation Score (RVS), a complementary evaluation metric that quantifies the extent to which a predictive model respects a given set of logical rules, independently of predictive accuracy. RVS treats hard rules (strict constraints) and soft rules (statistical regularities) differently, can be evaluated on any dataset and on any predictive model expressed over a relational vocabulary, and can be computed using SQL queries that are automatically generated for Horn rules. Beyond evaluating models, RVS can also evaluate the logical consistency of training datasets and help identify poorly defined rules. We evaluate RVS on three benchmarks covering knowledge graph link prediction and relational regression, including rule-based, embedding-based, and neuro-symbolic predictive models. Our results demonstrate that two models achieving comparable predictive accuracy can exhibit substantially different levels of logical compliance, revealing differences in model behavior that standard metrics fail to capture.

</details>


### [216] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种让大模型通过引入“良知步骤”自我审查并结合DPO进行在线对齐的新机制，成功实现了模型在复杂对抗场景下的自我伦理修正与“涌现对齐”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在复杂场景（特别是微调后）容易出现涌现式的非伦理行为（如代码攻击），研究旨在探索LLM是否具备自我感知且能通过自我修正来提升伦理水平。

Method: 1. 引入“良知步骤”（conscience step）：让模型在输出前自我审查逻辑与内容；2. 结合DPO（直接偏好优化）：通过对比学习将模型训练导向更符合伦理的方向；3. 自我评价机制：利用模型的冻结副本作为判别器，无需外部辅助模型。

Result: 提出了一种通用的在线对齐技术，在训练、微调、对抗性提示和零样本学习等多种场景下均有效，并成功在代码攻击场景中实现了从“涌现不一致”到“涌现对齐”的转变。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过引入“良知步骤”（conscience step）和基于DPO的在线对齐技术，模型能够实现“涌现对齐”（Emergent Alignment），即使在复杂的恶意攻击场景下，通过高级的反思性提问也能有效引导模型遵守伦理。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [217] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ITNet 通过单一可学习积分变换框架统一了卷积、RNN 和 Transformer 三大架构，证明了这些模型本质上是同一数学对象的不同变体，并在多项任务中展现出卓越的通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主流神经网络架构（CNN、RNN、Transformer）虽然在数学上被视为相互独立，但本文认为它们只是单一数学对象——“可学习积分变换”——在不同约束下的不完整表现形式。作者旨在通过统一的架构框架消除这种人为的架构碎片化。

Method: 提出了“积分变换网络”（ITNet），核心是一个由轻量级 MLP 实现的可学习核函数，该核函数同时依赖位置和特征。通过平铺核融合（Tiled kernel fusion）、重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分和可学习低秩分解等技术，解决了该架构在计算上的可扩展性问题。

Result: ITNet 作为通用连续算子近似器，不仅在理论上涵盖了卷积、自注意力及各类递归模型，在实验中也证明其能够以单一架构在图像（ImageNet）、语言（GLUE）、建模（ModelNet40）和视觉问答（VQA/NLVR2）等跨模态基准测试上达到或超越现有专门设计的模型。

Conclusion: 研究证明，卷积、递归和注意力机制并非数学上截然不同的架构，而是单一潜在算子（可学习积分变换）的不同特例。ITNet 通过统一架构实现了对这些多样化行为的覆盖，证明了单一交互机制足以从数据中学习并恢复出所有三种架构的特性，无需针对性设计。
