Building on the observation that conditional flow matching (CFM) can disentangle condition-controlled factors from instance-specific residuals, our framework directly operates in residual space and separates two complementary search regimes: self-pollination performs local exploitation through feature-preserving residual refinement, and cross-pollination promotes broader exploration by recombining residuals across heterogeneous samples. As a proof of concept, we validate on MorphoMNIST, a benchmark dataset for counterfactual generation, and on crystal data, demonstrating that this exploration--exploitation decomposition provides a useful mechanism for balancing target alignment, instance preservation, and diversity, and extends beyond images to real-world scientific domains.

</details>


### [253] [Multi-Head Attention-Based Feature Extractor Integration with Soft Actor-Critic for Porosity Prediction and Process Parameter Optimization in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20087)
*Kianoush Aqabakee,Leonardo Stella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成多头注意力机制的SAC强化学习框架，有效提升了激光粉末床熔融增材制造中工艺参数优化的收敛速度与系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于离散动作空间的强化学习方法在处理高精度增材制造任务时存在收敛慢、易陷入局部最优的问题，难以满足精密参数控制需求。

Method: 采用连续动作空间架构，将多头注意力（Multi-head Attention）机制集成进软演员-评论家（Soft Actor-Critic, SAC）算法中，利用注意力机制增强对低维输入特征细微变化的捕捉能力。

Result: 在激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）的孔隙率预测与参数优化验证中，该算法在14个回合内达到322.79的收敛值，性能优于DQN、PPO、TD3及标准SAC等主流方法。

Conclusion: 提出的基于多头注意力机制的SAC算法在增材制造过程优化中表现优于传统强化学习方法，不仅实现了更快的收敛速度，还保持了更高的训练稳定性，为解决激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）中的工艺参数调控与缺陷控制提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing process optimization requires precise parameter control to minimize defects such as porosity. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) approaches using discrete action spaces suffer from slow convergence and susceptibility to local optima, limiting their effectiveness for high-precision manufacturing tasks. This study addresses these limitations by employing a continuous action space combined with a novel architecture that integrates a multi-head attention mechanism with the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm. The attention-based feature extractor enhances the agent's ability to capture subtle variations in low-dimensional input features, enabling more effective exploration-exploitation balance for navigating value spaces with local minima. We validate our approach on porosity prediction and process parameter optimization in laser powder bed fusion, demonstrating faster convergence and higher final reward values compared to standard RL methods including DQN, PPO, TD3, and vanilla SAC. The proposed methodology achieves a convergence value of 322.79 within 14 episodes, outperforming existing approaches while maintaining stability throughout training.

</details>


### [254] [ScaffoldAgent: Utility-Guided Dynamic Outline Optimization for Open-Ended Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20122)
*Zhibang Yang,Xinke Jiang,Yuzhen Xiao,Ruizhe Zhang,Yue Fang,XinFei Wan,Zhengxing Song,Yuxuan Liu,Yuheng Huang,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对开放式深度研究中大纲难以动态适应信息积累的问题，ScaffoldAgent引入了一种效用引导的动态优化框架，通过结构化的决策过程和反馈机制，显著提升了长文报告的生成质量与事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OEDR方法在大纲管理上存在局限：预定义大纲缺乏灵活性，而基于局部启发式的动态更新易产生“脚手架偏移”且评估反馈滞后。

Method: 提出了ScaffoldAgent框架，将大纲演化建模为包含“扩展、收缩、修订”三种操作的结构化决策过程，并引入了基于检索增益、结构相干性和试生成质量的效用反馈机制，用于指导推理过程中的大纲决策。

Result: 在DeepResearch Bench和DeepResearch Gym上的实验表明，ScaffoldAgent在长文报告生成质量和事实准确性方面均优于现有的深度研究代理。

Conclusion: ScaffoldAgent通过效用引导的动态大纲优化，有效解决了开放式深度研究（OEDR）中大纲偏移导致的问题，证明了结构化大纲更新对高质量长文报告生成的重要性。

Abstract: Open-ended deep research (OEDR) requires systems to acquire knowledge through multi-round retrieval and generate coherent long-form reports. The outline plays a central role as a structural scaffold that coordinates retrieval, evidence organization, and generation. However, existing methods either fix the outline before writing or refine it with local heuristics, leading to scaffold drift under continuous information accumulation and delayed feedback for evaluating outline modifications. We propose ScaffoldAgent, a utility-guided dynamic outline optimization framework for OEDR. ScaffoldAgent models outline evolution as a structured decision process with three operations: Expansion, Contraction, and Revision, enabling controlled updates to the report scaffold. It further introduces a utility-guided feedback mechanism that estimates the downstream value of each outline operation from retrieval gain, structural coherence, and trial-generation quality. The resulting utility signal guides node selection, operation scheduling, and termination during inference. Experiments on DeepResearch Bench and DeepResearch Gym show that ScaffoldAgent consistently improves long-form report generation and factual grounding over existing deep research agents.

</details>


### [255] [Learning to Prompt: Improving Student Engagement with Adaptive LLM-based High-School Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20138)
*Po-Chin Chang,Nicholas Hogan,Aske Plaat,Michiel T. van der Meer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于教学特征的自适应提示路由系统，通过动态调整教学策略显著提升了大模型在个性化辅导中的教学效率与学生转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于静态提示（Static-prompt）的大模型辅导系统难以适应多学科领域及学生多样化的学习需求，缺乏个性化的教学策略调适能力。

Method: 1. 提取14个教学特征（如支架式教学、学生理解度等）作为系统基础；2. 训练一个提示路由模型（Prompt Router），先在仿真环境中预训练，再部署于高中心理学/教育场景；3. 通过A/B测试验证从仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）的迁移能力，对比贪婪策略与随机抽样策略的效果。

Result: 1. 仿真测试中路由模型优于静态基线（0.694 vs 0.647/0.64）；2. 真实场景中，系统显著提升了效率，平均减少约3轮对话（p=0.007）；3. 相比静态 baseline，随机路由策略大幅提升了练习转换率（28.1% vs 19.6%）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于教学特征的自适应提示路由机制（Prompt Routing）能有效提升大模型在个性化教育中的表现。相比静态提示，动态选择教学策略（如从分析转向支架式教学）能显著提高教学效率，在维持教学质量的同时减少对话轮次，且随机路由策略在促进学生练习转换率方面表现更优。

Abstract: LLMs can personalize education, although current static-prompt tutoring systems struggle to adapt to diverse academic disciplines. We develop and test a system with subject-aware prompting, based on 14 pedagogical features (e.g., tutor scaffolding, student understanding) extracted from raw transcripts. We first train a prompt routing model in a simulation environment, and then deploy it for online adaptation with actual high-school students. The simulation benchmark shows the router outperforming two static baselines ($0.694$ vs. $0.647$ and $0.64$, $p<0.001$). A/B testing ($N=656$ conversations from 359 students) shows sim-to-real transfer where the model switches from analytical to scaffolding learning strategies. Our adaptive prompt selection mechanism improves instructional efficiency, maintains pedagogical quality and reduces interactions by around 3 turns ($p=0.007$). While a greedy router achieves a comparable exercise conversion rate with the baseline ($19.1\%$ vs. $19.6\%$), a stochastic router that samples strategies leads to a higher conversion rate ($28.1\%$).

</details>


### [256] [RACL: Reasoning-Agent Control Layers for Continuous Metaheuristic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20142)
*Antón Asla Manzárraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RACL，一种能够动态调整和优化元启发式算法搜索行为的推理智能体层；它无需修改原始优化器及业务限制，通过自动发现、验证和整合控制策略，显著提升了算法性能并赋予了其可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在克服传统元启发式算法在复杂场景中控制逻辑固化、缺乏自适应性以及决策“黑盒”等问题，为元启发式算法提供一种能够解释且自我进化的控制机制。

Method: 提出 RACL（推理智能体控制层），通过观察操作内存、基于历史行为推理、构建/验证有限假设、评估结果并实施策略，动态控制现有优化器的搜索行为。文中以车辆路径问题为实验场景，利用 Codex 作为推理智能体尝试介入。

Result: 在实验中，RACL 在 21 个案例中均优于或持平操作内存策略（OMP），在 18 个案例中优于或持平停滞触发策略（STP）；在特定测试集下，平均成本较固定策略降低 8.337%，较 STP 降低 1.605%，且无显著计算开销。

Conclusion: RACL 是一种有效的元启发式算法控制框架，它通过引入推理层，在不改变业务约束的前提下，实现了对优化器搜索行为的自我优化、策略发现与决策解释，具有良好的通用性和实用潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces RACL, a Reasoning-Agent Control Layer for metaheuristics. RACL places a reasoning agent above an existing optimizer. The agent does not replace the optimizer and does not modify business constraints. Instead, it controls the optimizer's internal search behavior by observing operational memory, reasoning over past behavior, formulating bounded hypotheses, testing interventions, evaluating outcomes, applying guardrails, consolidating useful policies and explaining its decisions. The experiment uses vehicle routing as a testbed, but the contribution is not a new routing solver, a particular ALNS configuration or a specific set of routing rules. The contribution is the RACL method: a way for a reasoning agent to discover, validate, consolidate and explain algorithmic control rules for a metaheuristic. In the current experimental setting, RACL improves or ties the Operational Memory Policy in 21 of 21 feasible cases and improves or ties a non-reasoning Stagnation-Triggered Policy in 18 of 21 feasible cases, with an average RACL vs STP cost delta of -0.641%. In the Sevilla-9/10 runtime sample, RACL improves average cost by -8.337% versus Fixed and -1.605% versus STP without showing material computational overhead. During the proof-of-concept, Codex was used as an in-the-loop reasoning agent observing executions, interpreting logs and proposing live bounded interventions. The policy proxy was later used only to make quantitative evaluation reproducible.

</details>


### [257] [BIM-Edit: Benchmarking Large Language Models for IFC-Based Building Information Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20146)
*Bharathi Kannan Nithyanantham,Clemens Kujat,Tobias Sesterhenn,Stefan Telgmann,Jörn Plönnigs,Stefan Lüdtke,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了 BIM-Edit 基准测试，旨在评估 LLM 在建筑信息模型（BIM）中的自然语言编辑能力。研究发现，当前主流 LLM 处理涉及空间与拓扑关系的工程编辑任务时能力有限，距离实际工程应用仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CAD 测试基准主要集中于生成新模型，忽视了工程实践中对现有模型进行精确编辑、语义保留及关系维护的需求，缺乏针对复杂建筑信息模型（BIM/IFC）的评估标准。

Method: 提出了 BIM-Edit 基准测试，包含 324 个编辑任务（涵盖 11 个真实建筑模型和 36 个合成场景），构建了直接、空间、拓扑三类指示，并从几何精度、语义有效性、拓扑一致性三个维度对 LLM 进行评估。

Result: 实验显示表现最好的 LLM 在三个维度上的平均得分仅为 49.5%，完全解决任务的比例不超过 3.4%。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型（LLM）在建筑信息模型（BIM）的复杂编辑任务中表现欠佳，难以满足工程设计流程中对几何精确性、语义有效性和拓扑一致性的严苛要求，存在巨大的技术缺口。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to computer-aided design (CAD) to generate design artifacts from textual instructions. In engineering practice, this requires more than creating new geometry, models must also understand existing scenes, edit them correctly, and preserve semantics and relations. However, many CAD benchmarks focus on creating new models rather than editing existing ones, and mostly evaluate geometric correctness. We introduce BIM-Edit, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on natural-language editing of Building Information Models (BIM) represented in the Industry Foundation Classes (IFC) format. BIM provides a challenging testbed because building models encode geometry together with semantic and relational structure. BIM-Edit contains 324 editing tasks spanning 11 realistic building models and 36 synthetic scenes. Tasks are expressed using three instruction categories - direct, spatial, and topological - covering both explicit and scene-grounded edits. We evaluate outputs along three dimensions: geometric accuracy, semantic validity, and topological consistency. Across evaluated LLMs, the best-performing model achieves only 49.5% average score across the three metrics, and no model fully solves more than 3.4% of tasks. These results demonstrate a substantial gap between current LLM capabilities and the requirements of structured engineering design workflows.

</details>


### [258] [Modularity-Free Conflict-Averse Training for Generalized PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20156)
*Heejo Kong,Beomchul Park,Sung-Jin Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对过参数化 PINN 中出现的“功能模块化”导致的训练失效问题，提出了 ModSync 框架，通过结构约束机制强化跨目标交互，有效提升了 PINN 的训练稳定性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的冲突规避优化方案在训练 PINN 时表现良好，但这些方法在模型容量增加时效果会变差。作者发现过参数化网络会导致“功能模块化”，即网络自发分割为互不干扰的任务独占模块，导致无法实现跨目标交互，从而阻碍收敛。

Method: 提出了“模块化稀疏同步”（ModSync）框架。该方法通过惩罚任务独占连接（task-exclusive connections）并保留促进交互的路径，将结构优化集成到冲突规避训练中。

Result: 在多个广泛的 PDE 基准测试中，ModSync 成功防止了容量驱动的失效，维持了稳健的跨目标耦合，并达到了 SOTA（最先进）的精度。

Conclusion: ModSync 将结构优化与冲突规避训练相结合，通过惩罚任务独占连接并保留交互路径，有效克服了过参数化 PINN 中的容量诱导失效问题，实现了卓越的收敛性和精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have become a powerful framework for solving PDEs by embedding physical laws into differentiable objectives. Despite their advances, training PINNs remains fragile: recent conflict-averse optimization schemes alleviate gradient interference between residual and boundary losses, but we show that their effectiveness deteriorates as model capacity increases. In this paper, we identify a capacity-induced failure mode, where overparameterized networks undergo functional modularity, self-partitioning into task-exclusive modules that suppress cross-objective interaction and hinder convergence toward Pareto-stationary points. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework, Modular-Sparsity Synchronization (ModSync), which integrates structural optimization into conflict-averse training by penalizing task-exclusive connections while preserving interaction-promoting pathways. Extensive experiments across diverse PDE benchmarks demonstrate that ModSync consistently prevents capacity-driven failures, sustains robust cross-objective coupling, and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. Codes are available at \url{https://github.com/heejokong/ModSync}.

</details>


### [259] [Implicit Semantic-Aware Communication Based on Hypergraph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20162)
*Yiwei Liao,Shurui Tu,Yong Xiao,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HISR 框架，利用超图建模复杂高阶语义关系，通过子空间解耦提升了语义通信在面对噪声和信息丢失时的推理鲁棒性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于简单图（仅支持成对关系）的语义通信方法忽略了现实场景中广泛存在的多实体高阶关联，导致语义表达受限，在复杂或受损信道下推理性能下降且易产生歧义。

Method: 提出了一种基于超图的隐式语义推理框架 (HISR)，利用超图建模复杂的多实体高阶关系；将实体及其关联映射至特定的语义子空间以解耦交互并缓解过平滑问题。

Result: 数值实验表明，HISR 的隐式语义解释准确率较当前最先进基准提升了最高 36.6%。

Conclusion: HISR 框架通过引入超图结构，有效捕捉了多实体间的高阶语义关联，在增强语义表达能力的同时，显著提升了在噪声或信息丢失环境下的推断鲁棒性与准确度。

Abstract: Semantic-aware communication has emerged as a transformative paradigm for next-generation communication systems, shifting the fundamental goal from transmitting bit-level symbols to reliably recovering and understanding the semantic meaning of information. Previous studies have demonstrated that representing the semantic content of source messages as graph-based structures can significantly improve communication efficiency and the accuracy of semantic inference at the receiver. However, existing solutions typically employ graphs that capture only pairwise relationships, thereby neglecting higher-order implicit correlations commonly observed in real-world scenarios, such as group interactions, multi-entity associations, and complex relational contexts. This limitation reduces semantic expressiveness and makes semantic inference susceptible to ambiguity and performance degradation, particularly under noisy or corrupted channel conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel hypergraph-based implicit semantic reasoning framework, HISR, which leverages hypergraphs to represent complex multi-entity relationships among semantic knowledge entities. In HISR, entities and their associated higher-order relations are mapped into dedicated semantic subspaces tailored to distinct relational contexts. This design not only disentangles diverse semantic interactions to mitigate the over-smoothing effects commonly found in traditional graph embedding methods but also enables robust semantic inference even when partial information loss occurs during transmission. Numerical results show that the proposed HISR achieves up to a 36.6% improvement in implicit semantic interpretation accuracy over the state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [260] [Apparent Psychological Profiles of Large Language Models are Largely a Measurement Artifact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20205)
*Jelena Meyer,David Garcia,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究指出，大语言模型的心理画像大多是测量工具产生的伪影，而非模型自身属性，主要是由LLM普遍存在的“方向性响应偏见”导致的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被赋予稳定的心理画像，并被用于决策、安全性评估和作为人类参与者的代理。研究动机在于探究这些所谓的“心理特征”是否具有科学严谨性或仅仅是测量误差。

Method: 研究者利用心理测量学框架，对56个经过指令微调的LLM进行了广泛的心理测试（涵盖人格和风险偏好量表，包括自评和行为任务），并将结果与大规模人类参考样本进行对比分析。采用了方差分解法和响应正交性分析等统计手段。

Result: 1. LLM的模型间差异主要由“方向性响应偏见”驱动（占变异的81-90%），远高于人类（9-16%）；2. 这种偏见随模型能力提升而下降，但无法彻底消除；3. 量表的表观信度几乎完全由响应正交性决定；4. 模型表现出的画像可随题目筛选而随意改变。

Conclusion: 目前大语言模型（LLM）所呈现的心理特征并非模型固有的属性，而是心理测量工具（为人类设计）在LLM上应用时产生的一种测量伪影。研究呼吁开发专门针对LLM的、以“响应正交性（response orthogonality）”为核心的评估框架。

Abstract: Psychological instruments designed for humans are increasingly used to assign large language models (LLMs) stable psychological profiles that affect their usability, safety assessment, and use as proxies for human participants in research. Using a formal psychometric framework, we show that these profiles are largely a measurement artifact. Administering a battery of personality and risk-preference instruments spanning self-reports and behavioral tasks to 56 instruction-tuned LLMs alongside large human reference samples, we report four findings. First, differences between models are driven not by the traits an instrument targets but by a directional response bias, a tendency to respond toward one end of the scale, or one labeled option, regardless of item content; a variance decomposition attributes 81-90% of between-model variation to this bias, against 9-16% in humans. Second, the bias declines with model capability but is not eliminated by it. Third, because bias rather than trait drives responding, an instrument's apparent reliability is almost entirely predicted by its response orthogonality, a term we coin for the proportion of items for which trait and bias point in opposite directions. Fourth, the profile a model appears to have shifts with the items used and can be manufactured through item selection. These results demonstrate that the apparent psychological profiles of LLMs are artifacts of the instrument used to measure them, not properties of the models themselves. As instruments borrowed from human psychology are rarely fully orthogonal and may inherently lack validity for LLMs, we call for dedicated assessments centered on response orthogonality.

</details>


### [261] [Augmenting Game AI with Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20210)
*Alessandro Sestini,Joakim Bergdahl,Amir Baghi,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Florian Fuchs,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用强化学习（RL）替代传统手工编码系统来提升游戏AI真实感的方法，并提出了一套实用的强化学习训练框架，指出了当前部署面临的技术挑战与未来研究路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手工编码的游戏系统难以实现复杂的行为，限制了角色真实感，导致玩家沉浸感下降；作者旨在探讨如何利用机器学习（特别是强化学习）克服这些挑战，创造更逼真、可信的游戏角色。

Method: 1. 提出一套针对游戏AI开发和需求定制的强化学习模型训练框架；2. 通过典型游戏案例演示强化学习增强AI的应用；3. 分析在现代游戏中部署面向玩家的机器学习代理的具体实践；4. 识别当前技术瓶颈并指明未来的研究方向。

Result: 构建了一个适用于游戏开发的强化学习训练框架，不仅展示了该技术在实际游戏中的部署可行性，还总结了一系列关键技术挑战，为推动机器学习在游戏产业中的落地提供了指导。

Conclusion: 强化学习在游戏AI中具有巨大潜力，但需解决当前研究中的局限性。本文提出的开发框架及明确的研究瓶颈为游戏行业广泛应用机器学习提供了方向，以实现更具沉浸感的角色行为。

Abstract: Immersion in video games depends not only on graphics, audio, and game mechanics, but also on the quality of in-game characters. Producing believable characters, or game AI, remains a significant challenge as behavioral complexity is hard to capture with hand-coded systems. Game AI is a source of immersion and engagement; however, the limitations stemming from the challenges of creating game AI often lead to frustration and the breaking of the illusion of realism within the game. The introduction of machine learning models opens the door to creating more believable, authentic, and relatable characters in games. The promise is that they either learn from interacting with the game, or from player data, to develop true human-like behavior. In this paper, we envision more applications of reinforcement learning for game AI in the future. For this to materialize, current research limitations are prohibitive to broad deployment across game genres. Therefore, we propose a framework for training reinforcement learning models with a set of requirements in mind that are suited towards game AI and game development. We present examples of games with reinforcement learning-augmented game AI and describe the practicalities of deploying player-facing machine learning agents in modern games. Furthermore, we identify bottlenecks and hard problems in these areas, which we believe offer promising research directions to accelerate the adoption of machine learning in game AI for the video game industry.

</details>


### [262] [QMFOL: Benchmarking Large Language Model Reasoning via Quantifiable Monadic First-Order Logic Test Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20227)
*Xinyi Zheng,Ling Shi,Tianlong Yu,Yongxin Zhao,Lorenz Goette,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 QMFOL 框架，用于自动化生成具有定量逻辑复杂度的演绎推理任务（QMFOLBench），通过实验揭示了当前主流大模型在处理复杂逻辑时的性能弱点与敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演绎推理基准测试缺乏对逻辑复杂度的精细化控制，难以平衡逻辑一致性与语义多样性，无法满足大模型快速迭代后的评估需求。

Method: 提出了自动化框架 QMFOL，利用一阶谓词逻辑（FOL）构建可定量控制复杂度（深度、宽度、标签类型等）的逻辑结构，并结合 LLM 进行自然语言转换及外部逻辑求解器进行往返一致性校验。

Result: 通过 QMFOLBench 测试发现，模型性能随逻辑复杂度增加而下降，且对标签类型（True/False/Unknown）存在偏见，对语义变化敏感，表现出计算开销随复杂度上升的趋势。

Conclusion: 提出 QMFOL 框架和 QMFOLBench 基准，为评估大模型的演绎推理能力提供了更具可控性、可扩展性和可靠性的定量分析工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in reasoning, particularly in deductive reasoning, which is crucial for high-stakes decision-making. As models improve, evaluation benchmarks should evolve to keep pace. However, existing benchmarks lack fine-grained control over logical complexity and struggle to balance semantic diversity with logical consistency.
  To address these issues, we propose QMFOL, an automated framework for generating monadic first-order logic reasoning tasks with quantifiable and controllable complexity. It constructs formal logical structures using conjunction and disjunction patterns, enabling precise control over reasoning depth, width, label types, and distractors. These structures are then translated into natural language via LLMs, with logical consistency ensured through round-trip verification using an external prover. Based on our framework, we build QMFOLBench, a benchmark comprising 2880 instances with 960 configurations across diverse logical and semantic dimensions. Evaluations on six large reasoning models (LRMs) and two LLMs show that performance degrades and computational overhead increases with rising logical complexity. Models perform better on True-labeled tasks than on False or Unknown ones, and exhibit sensitivity to semantic variation. Overall, QMFOL offers a scalable and reliable approach for constructing deductive reasoning benchmarks with controllable complexity, enabling more precise evaluation of reasoning capabilities in modern language models.

</details>


### [263] [Thermodynamic Measure of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20231)
*Ishanu Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于热力学的智能度量框架，认为智能本质上是递归地放大“稀有但有效”的未来，并证明了递归自模拟是实现高级智能的必要且近充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决智能可度量性的核心问题，通过物理学（特别是热力学和信息论）视角，为智能提供一个统一的定义和衡量标准。

Method: 通过热力学框架，将智能定义为“稀有但有效未来”（rare but valid futures）的合法放大（lawful amplification），并论证了递归自模拟（recursive self-simulation）在该过程中的必要性与近充分性。

Result: 证明了高“稀有有效提升”（rare-valid lift）不仅依赖于系统对稀有有效未来的高保真建模，且当具备有效策略时，系统的表现可接近受作用限制的最优解。

Conclusion: 递归自模拟不仅是智能的特征，更是实现高热力学智能的基础；该框架提供了一种跨越物质、AI及生物系统的通用智能度量方式。

Abstract: Can intelligence be measured? We propose that intelligence can be defined as the lawful amplification of rare but valid futures: a system increases the probability of outcomes that would be unlikely under passive dynamics but remain admissible under the constraints of the domain. We start with the premise that an intelligent system must model the world and its own place within it. Because the system is part of the world it models, this leads naturally to recursive self-simulation: the system represents futures in which its own actions are part of the trajectory. Our central results give a necessity statement and a conditional near-sufficiency statement connecting this architecture to a precise thermodynamic measure of lawful amplification of rare-valid futures: high rare-valid lift is impossible unless the internal simulation identifies rare-valid futures with high fidelity; conversely, when rare-valid fidelity is high and the simulation contains an effective policy, the achievable lift approaches the actuation-limited optimum. Thus recursive self-simulation is not merely a plausible feature of intelligence but, under the stated assumptions, is necessary and nearly sufficient for high thermodynamic intelligence. The resulting framework makes intelligence measurable on a universal scale, from passive matter and feedback controllers, large language models, and humans as text generators to Maxwell-demon-like information engines.

</details>


### [264] [A Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20236)
*Federica Filippini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对动态环境下强化学习处理约束优化时权重调节困难的问题，论文提出了MAMO框架，通过多智能体强化学习实现惩罚权重的自动化调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统强化学习在处理带约束的成本最小化问题时，依赖人工手动调整惩罚权重，导致在非平稳环境下难以平衡目标优化与约束违规的问题。

Method: 提出了MAMO（Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization）框架，通过多智能体强化学习机制，将奖励函数中惩罚项权重的动态自动调节视为一个独立的可学习任务，而非手动设置权重。

Result: 实现了任务执行与目标权重设计的解耦，使得系统能够根据环境变化自动适应权重，提高了约束优化问题的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体强化学习将奖励权重选择问题解耦为独立的学习任务，提供了一种在动态环境下更具自主性和鲁棒性的约束优化解决方案。

Abstract: Many decision-making problems in computing and networking systems can be naturally formulated as cost-minimization problems under performance constraints. In dynamic environments, reinforcement learning (RL) is often used to solve such problems at runtime by embedding both costs and constraint violations into a single scalar reward through weighted penalty terms, following a Lagrangian-inspired formulation. However, in this context the behavior of the learned policy critically depends on the choice of these weights, which are typically selected manually. This makes it difficult to identify an appropriate trade-off between optimizing the primary objective and effectively avoiding constraint violations, particularly in non-stationary environments where their relative importance may change. This paper presents MAMO (Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization), an approach to tackle this balancing problem through multi-agent RL. MAMO decouples task execution from objective design by formulating the selection of reward weights as a learning problem, providing a !rst step towards more autonomous and robust RL-based solutions for constrained optimization problems in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [265] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型中内部知识与外部信息间的冲突问题，本文提出了MACR框架。该方法通过量化模型置信度进行自适应评估，并利用多智能体推理系统主动识别与解决知识矛盾，实现了更稳健、可解释的冲突处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理知识冲突时往往盲目信任单一来源（模型或上下文），缺乏主动解决不一致问题的机制，且忽略了两个来源均可能出错的情况。

Method: 提出MACR（Multi-Agent Conflict Resolution）框架：1. 使用基于修正语义熵的自适应知识评估与检索方法量化模型置信度；2. 建立包含三个专门智能体（规则归纳、冲突分析、一致性解决）的归纳式多智能体推理流程。

Result: 实证结果表明，MACR在多项基准测试中显著优于现有最先进的基线模型，并能提供冲突解决过程的解释。

Conclusion: MACR框架通过多智能体推理有效解决了LLM中内部知识与外部知识以及外部知识之间的冲突问题，在处理知识冲突任务中表现优异且具有良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [266] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ViT的置信度感知框架，用于自动化评分学生的科学绘图，通过自动处理高置信度回答并保留不确定结果进行人工审阅，实现了高效且可靠的教育评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生创作的科学绘图是评估概念理解的重要工具，但传统人工评分模式在大规模课堂应用中成本高昂且难以持续，需开发自动评分系统以解决这一挑战。

Method: 利用视觉Transformer (ViT) 模型，结合参数高效微调 (PEFT) 技术，并引入置信度感知评分框架，通过测试时预测分布评估模型对学生绘图的评分置信度，实现自动评分与人工复核的协同机制。

Result: 在6项NGSS（新一代科学标准）相关的初中评估任务中，该模型表现出良好的性能，能够通过自动筛选高置信度结果、重定向不确定案例至人工审阅，显著提升了评分的可靠性与实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的置信度感知自动评分框架能够有效平衡自动化覆盖率与评分风险，验证了该方法在教育评估中实现高效且可靠自动化评分的可行性。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [267] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lagrange 是一种新型自动驾驶框架，通过结合视觉语言模型与连续能量场优化，在无需密集重建的前提下，实现了具备开放词汇理解能力且符合车辆运动学约束的决策控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶范式在“表示效率”与“泛化能力”之间存在权衡：密集模型计算压力大，稀疏查询模型受限于封闭集，而 VLA 模型因离散标记生成难以满足高频控制需求。需要一种既能实现开放词汇推理又符合车辆运动学的轻量化架构。

Method: 提出名为 Lagrange 的端到端自动驾驶框架，核心包括：1. 基于屏蔽潜在场 (Masked Latent Fields, MLF)；2. 利用视觉语言模型 (VLM) 将类无关对象提案编码为连续语义视觉标记；3. 引入意图驱动的屏蔽交叉注意力模块进行空间过滤；4. 将决策过程构架为连续能量场上的拉格朗日动作最小化问题。

Result: 在 nuScenes（标准）和 CODA（长尾）数据集的离线评估中，Lagrange 在复杂、开放世界的场景下表现出优异的鲁棒性和运动学一致性。

Conclusion: Lagrange 框架通过将决策建模为拉格朗日动作最小化问题，成功结合了开放词汇推理与物理动力学约束，实现了一种鲁棒、可解释且符合运动学的自动驾驶新方案。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [268] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对数据匮乏环境下的机械故障诊断，本文提出了一种结合周期性多激励机制及新型数据增强技术的深度迁移学习方法，在铁路受电弓实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习（DTL）应用于智能故障诊断系统（IFDS）时，通常严重依赖大规模标注数据，而在机械结构故障场景下获取此类数据存在巨大挑战。

Method: 提出了一种利用真实系统固有非线性特征的周期性多激励水平程序，通过将其转化为图像数据，利用预训练卷积神经网络（CNN）进行分类诊断；同时引入了针对故障诊断系统的新型数据可视化及增强技术。

Result: 在铁路受电弓结构的实验验证中，该方法表现出良好的诊断效果，有效缓解了数据匮乏对深层模型训练的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的利用周期性多激励水平程序和新型数据增强技术的方法，能够在数据极其匮乏的情况下，有效地实现基于振动的智能故障诊断。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [269] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SoftSkill框架，通过将自然语言技能压缩为紧凑的软提示（Soft Prompts），在保持骨干模型冻结的前提下，实现了更高效且性能更佳的智能体行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Markdown的自然语言技能存在依赖大型模型进行实时解析、推理成本高且效率低的问题；作者旨在探讨能否用紧凑的连续上下文对象替代长文本技能，并将技能转化为冻结模型可执行的潜在行为先验。

Method: 提出SoftSkill方法：将自然语言技能转化为长度为32的连续软前缀（soft prefix），通过冻结骨干模型并仅对软提示进行训练（next-token prediction），在推理时将其作为潜在的行为先验使用。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上，SoftSkill在SearchQA、LiveMath和DocVQA任务上表现优异，相比无技能提示和SkillOpt方法均有显著提升；同时将数千个词元压缩为32个虚拟词元。但在长期任务的模仿学习方面仍存在局限。

Conclusion: 将技能转化为紧凑的潜在控制（Soft Skills）比将其编码为冗长的Markdown文本更有效，能够以更低的计算成本显著提升冻结骨干模型在各类任务中的表现。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [270] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 虽然从交互数据中挖掘出的技能具有高度可解释性，但目前的挖掘方法在转化为下游策略能力的提升上仍然受限，该研究揭示了现有技术在跨域应用中的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式技能库有助于提升计算机控制代理（Computer-using agents）的可解释性，但目前尚不清楚是否能通过挖掘交互数据来自动构建此类库，并有效提升下游策略性能。

Method: 提出一个三阶段工作流：1. GUI轨迹分割；2. 将片段聚类为候选技能；3. 基于聚类标签训练技能感知策略（Skill-aware policy）。

Result: 挖掘出的聚类具备较好的可读性（5个中有8个在InteraSkill基准测试中的纯度超过0.95），但在跨域任务中表现不佳，改进幅度极小，且在关键指标上表现甚至不如简单的频率先验。

Conclusion: 轨迹挖掘能够揭示可解释的技能结构，但现有的边界检测器、无序段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进，仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [271] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了主流E2M1格式在FP4训练中的“收缩偏差”缺陷，提出了一种基于均匀格式（E1M2/INT4）的UFP4训练配方，显著提升了大规模模型预训练的稳定性和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流硬件支持的FP4格式（E2M1）在预训练中存在固有的数学局限性，导致训练不稳定；研究旨在通过分析格式几何特征来优化4位训练的算法与效率。

Method: 1. 揭示E2M1格式中几何不对称导致的收缩偏差及经RHT放大后的负面影响；2. 提出UFP4训练配方：采用均匀4位格式（E1M2/INT4），对三大GEMM应用随机哈达玛变换（RHT），并将随机舍入限制在dY操作上。

Result: 在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B及MoE 124B的长期预训练任务中，UFP4相对于E2M1基准表现出更低的BF16相对损失降级。

Conclusion: 非均匀格式（如E2M1）在4位训练中存在固有的“收缩偏差（Shrinkage Bias）”，这是导致训练不稳定的根源。作者提出UFP4（基于均匀格式E1M2/INT4）能提供更好的训练稳定性与收敛效果，建议未来硬件应将均匀格式作为一等训练原语。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [272] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种结合 EfficientNet-B0 与 CBAM 注意力机制的精子形态分类框架，通过 Grad-CAM++ 提供决策可解释性，并在公开数据集上实现了高水平的分类准确度，具有极高的临床应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精子形态人工智能分析模型虽性能优异，但普遍缺乏可解释性，阻碍了其在临床诊断中的推广与应用，亟需开发既准确又透明的自动化分析工具。

Method: 提出了一个基于注意力机制的深度学习框架，整合了预训练的 EfficientNet-B0 和卷积块注意力模块 (CBAM)，并利用 Grad-CAM++ 技术进行决策过程的可视化。

Result: 在 SMIDS 和 HuSHem 公共数据集上分别达到了 90.2% 和 93.9% 的准确率（宏平均 F1 分数分别为 0.913 和 0.948），性能优于 SimpleCNN 和标准 EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将高精度模型与注意力机制及可视化技术结合，解决了深度学习在精子形态分析中的“黑箱”问题，为临床应用提供了可靠且可解释的自动化工具。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [273] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对实验室环境传感器数据进行动态特征工程，结合层级贝叶斯回归模型，显著提升了IVF妊娠率的预测准确度，并验证了环境监测信号的跨中心迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVF妊娠率预测通常仅依赖患者级变量，而忽略了实验室高分辨率环境数据的动态特性，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 1. 特征工程：从传感器原始数据中提取55个上下文感知的动态时间特征（如热稳定性、应力恢复速度等）；2. 统计建模：采用偏池化（partial pooling）的层级贝叶斯Beta回归模型，实现跨不同实验室（地理位置差异）的环境效应迁移学习。

Result: 在亚洲实验室数据中，预测误差从原始的3-5%降至1.27%；在北欧实验室的验证集中，模型达到R²=0.86，并使35-39岁组的预测误差降低了64%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，实验室微环境的动态时间特征包含具有高度临床意义且可迁移的预测信号，能够显著提升IVF妊娠率的预测精度。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [274] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文深入剖析了基于上下文学习的越狱机制，发现大语言模型对演示的解析受演示内容、顺序及偏好优化训练的多重制约，揭示了防御和攻击在上下文层面的深层相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知上下文演示能够诱导模型越狱（Jailbreak），但目前尚不清楚模型究竟如何解释不同类型的合规性演示，以及演示的组成方式如何影响模型的行为。

Method: 通过混合良性与有害的合规性演示进行实验，并针对四个不同大语言模型测试了三种关于演示组合如何驱动有害合规性的假设；同时分析了偏好优化训练阶段对防止良性演示诱导恶意行为的作用、演示顺序的近因效应，以及模型拒绝行为对上下文学习的影响。

Result: 1. 良性与有害演示不可互换，良性演示对有害合规性的影响受模型类型调制；2. 偏好优化（Preference Optimization）是抑制良性演示引发负面影响的关键；3. 演示执行存在显著的近因效应（Recency Bias）；4. 模型在拒绝回答时的行为差异巨大，部分模型保留了上下文格式，部分则彻底覆盖上下文信号。

Conclusion: 大语言模型如何响应上下文演示（In-context demonstrations）不仅取决于是否存在恶意示例，还受到演示内容本身、顺序偏见以及模型底层训练方式（如偏好优化）的复杂交互影响。这表明演示攻击并非简单的模式匹配，而是各种机制交织的结果。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [275] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multi-LCB 基准，将代码生成能力评估从单一的 Python 扩展至 12 种编程语言，评估结果显示当前 LLM 在多语言编程能力上存在显著缺陷和严重的不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 LiveCodeBench 仅局限于 Python 编程语言，无法全面衡量大模型在现实软件工程中所需的多语言泛化能力。

Method: 将 LiveCodeBench 中的 Python 任务转化为包含其他 11 种编程语言的等效任务，同时保留了原始的污染控制机制和评估协议，并利用该基准对 24 种大模型进行了指令遵循和推理能力的评估。

Result: 通过对 24 个大模型的测评，发现模型普遍存在对 Python 的过拟合、语言特异性的污染以及多语言性能之间的巨大差异。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB 是一个能够弥补现有 LiveCodeBench 局限性的多编程语言代码生成评估基准，揭示了当前大模型在多语言能力上的显著差距以及对 Python 的过度拟合问题。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [276] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit是一种针对冻结TTS模型的生命周期适应框架，利用Hopfield网络存储文本嵌入空间的纠错信息，无需重训练即可实时、高精度地修复生僻词读音错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本（zero-shot）流匹配TTS模型在部署后难以修正，特别是对生僻词（out-of-vocabulary）的读音错误，通常需要昂贵的重新训练才能解决。希望能实现一种无需改变模型权重即可进行持续适应的修正方法。

Method: 1. 提出FlowEdit框架：基于冻结的流匹配（Flow-matching）TTS，通过学习潜在空间中的文本嵌入扰动来纠正读音；
2. 存储机制：使用现代Hopfield网络（Modern Hopfield Network）作为内容可寻址的长期记忆库；
3. 推理机制：通过带有相似度门的软注意力机制进行检索，实现模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在包含18个语系、312个多语言专有名词的基准测试中，FlowEdit将目标词的音素错误率（PER）降低了92.7%，且保持了与原始模型相同的通用语音质量，单次修正推理仅需约15秒。

Conclusion: FlowEdit提供了一种无需微调、计算高效且高精度的生命周期适应框架，能够显著修复TTS模型中对专有名词的读音错误，同时保持原始模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [277] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP 提出了一种针对神经符号系统的高效单世界反事实推理框架，通过将神经感知与逻辑反事实转换融为一体，显著提升了推理效率并纠正了潜在的统计估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号系统（如 DeepProbLog）仅具备关联推断能力，缺乏处理干预（Intervention）和证据的反事实推理能力；基于双生网络（Twin networks）的传统反事实方法计算复杂度高。

Method: 引入 DeepSWIP 单世界反事实语义；利用神经物化（neural materialization）将神经谓词简化为 ProbLog 选择；通过加权模型计数（WMC）在单个转换后的程序上执行反事实计算。

Result: 在 MPI3D 实验中验证了与 DeepTwin 的精确性，且推理速度提升 2.14 倍；通过 SUMO HOV 实验证明，使用 AIPW 估计器可有效消除神经校准退化带来的统计偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP 提供了一种精确且高效的计数反事实推理方法，避免了双生网络（Twin Networks）的冗余计算。实验证明该方法在处理神经网络校准偏差和目标估计偏差方面表现优异。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [278] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LedgerAgent 是一种通过引入显式“账本”来管理任务状态和约束的推理方法，解决了传统提示词驱动智能体在状态维护和策略遵从方面的缺陷，显著提高了客户服务场景下的任务执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体设计通常缺乏显式的任务状态管理，导致模型依赖提示词中的混合信息进行推理，极易产生状态遗忘、错误引用或执行违反领域策略的工具调用等问题。

Method: 提出 LedgerAgent，在推理时为工具调用智能体维护一个显式的“账本”（Ledger）来存储任务状态。该账本不仅将状态信息显式地注入提示词中以辅助决策，还在执行变更环境的工具调用前，利用账本检查状态相关的策略约束以防止违规。

Result: 在四个客户服务领域的多项实验中，LedgerAgent 在各种开源和闭源模型上均提升了平均 pass@k 指标，且在严格的多轮一致性指标下表现出最大的增益。

Conclusion: LedgerAgent 通过引入显式状态管理和基于账本的策略执行，有效解决了现有工具调用智能体在多轮对话中状态维护不一致和策略违规的问题，显著提升了任务完成的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [279] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次将跨注意力归因技术引入语音扩散模型，揭示了自然语言提示词如何通过特定层级和采样阶段控制语音特征，为提升TTS系统的可解释性与控制力提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管风格化文字转语音（Style-captioned TTS）系统已能利用自然语言控制语音特征，但目前缺乏对各单词如何具体影响声学输出的机制性理解，导致模型的可控性差且难以诊断故障。

Method: 提出一种适用于语音扩散模型的跨注意力归因方法（Cross-attention attribution），将DAAM框架适配于语音领域，并将其应用于CapSpeech-TTS模型。通过在25个层和24个ODE步长上提取每个token的热力图进行量化分析。

Result: 1. 风格token的时间方差低于内容/功能token，验证了其作为全局调节器的定位；2. 风格注意力与基频（F0）和能量高度相关；3. 风格调节在扩散过程的早期步长和深层网络中达到峰值；4. 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，对应风格重要性的顶峰，反映了模型在关键阶段的最高选择性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过跨注意力归因证明了文本提示（style-caption）在语音扩散模型中并非随机映射，而是通过特定的层级和采样步骤精确调控语音的全局风格特征。研究澄清了风格描述词如何影响声学输出，为可解释的语音合成奠定了基础。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [280] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了MoE模型在分布偏移下的校准机制，指出专家校准在软路由模型中仍有缺陷，并提出了一种对抗性重加权策略以提升模型在极端情况下的校准与预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管专家级校准已被证明有助于提升混合专家模型（MoE）的性能，但其在分布偏移下发挥作用的具体条件和机制尚不明确。

Method: 1. 分析了专家校准与路由机制（硬路由 vs. 软路由）在分布偏移下的相互作用；2. 提出了一种对抗性重加权方法，通过惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差来优化模型。

Result: 该方法在各种模型类别、预测任务及不同分布偏移场景下，均有效改善了平均性能及困难数据子集上的准确率与校准权衡（accuracy-calibration tradeoff）。

Conclusion: 研究表明专家校准在硬路由模型中即足以保证整体校准，但在软路由模型中不足；提出的对抗性重加权方法能有效地提升模型在分布偏移下的准确率与校准性能均衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP是一个针对远程内存优化的数据库算子框架，通过在成本模型中引入传输轮次限制，显著降低了内存溢出时的网络往返开销，大幅提升了分析型查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的内存溢出处理策略主要针对磁盘I/O，旨在最小化总I/O量，但在远程内存环境下，这种策略会导致过多的传输轮次，从而受到高延迟的影响。

Method: 提出了一个远程内存感知（remote-memory-aware）的算子优化框架，将传输轮数纳入延迟成本模型，并针对阻塞式嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接推导出了特定的缓冲区划分策略。

Result: 在微基准测试中，传输轮次减少了高达97%，算子运行时间缩短了48%；在TPC-H和TPC-DS查询中，平均运行时间分别降低了22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: REMOP通过将传输轮次（transfer rounds）纳入成本模型，有效优化了分析型数据库在远程内存环境下的执行效率，显著降低了溢出（spill）查询的延迟。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [282] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL是一种扩展SQL的声明式决策语言，允许用户直接在数据库内定义决策变量、约束和优化目标，由数据库引擎自动求解最优方案，弥合了关系数据处理与复杂决策优化之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在现有关系数据中进行复杂决策（如资源分配、调度、最优子集选择等）时，传统SQL处理能力不足且通常需要将数据导出至外部优化器的问题，提出一种能够在数据库内直接表达和求解决策问题的声明式语言。

Method: 设计并定义了新的SQL扩展语法：包含用于定义决策空间（候选集）的'CREATE CANDIDATES'子句，以及用于声明决策变量、约束和目标的'DECIDE'子句；遵循声明式编程原则，由数据库引擎负责优化方案的求解。

Result: 本文档完成了DeQL的语言规范设计，涵盖语法、形式文法及执行模型，并通过多个应用场景（包括子集选择、分配、调度等）验证了其表达能力，同时支持不确定性优化和性能有界的求解。

Conclusion: DeQL通过扩展SQL，成功实现了在关系数据库中直接进行决策优化，将决策空间定义、约束与目标优化集成到标准SQL工作流中，证明了声明式决策语言在数据库系统中的可行性。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [283] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对云存储计费差异化（按量收费而非按延迟），本文提出以“美元成本”而非“命中率”为目标的缓存评估体系，通过开发cost-FOO离线最优边界，证明了GreedyDual等成本感知算法在缓解异构成本带来的经济损失方面优于LRU，并给出了衡量系统是否需要迁移至成本感知缓存的定量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云对象存储的计费模式（基于请求次数和流出流量，而非延迟）使得传统缓存策略（如LRU，专注于最小化失效率）在经济上并非最优。现有研究缺乏衡量现有启发式算法与实际美元成本最优解之间差距的基准。

Method: 1. 将缓存优化目标从“失效率(miss rate)”转变为“成本(dollars)”。
2. 对于固定大小对象，使用整数区间线性规划求解离线最优解；对于可变大小对象，通过流模型（cost-FOO）提供紧密的离线最优边界。
3. 利用真实Twitter负载数据，对比LRU与GreedyDual算法在不同定价模型下的性能差距。

Result: 1. 发现LRU的额外成本（dollar-regret）与负载的成本离散度呈强正相关。
2. GreedyDual算法能将成本遗憾降低至LRU的十分之一。
3. 定义了闭式解边界s*，用于预测特定负载是否必须采用成本感知缓存。
4. cost-FOO方法在不同场景下能保持约4%的精度，提供了一个有效的性能基准。

Conclusion: 传统的以“命中率”为目标的缓存策略在云存储场景下是欠优的；引入“以美元计价”的缓存算法（如GreedyDual）能显著降低成本。文中提出的s*临界点公式可作为评估是否需要采用成本感知缓存策略的有效依据。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


### [284] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对向量数据库缺乏细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的安全局限，本文提出了“策略感知向量搜索”框架，理清了在高性能向量检索中实现安全策略的机制与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代向量数据库在安全敏感型AI应用（如RAG）中应用广泛，但缺乏对细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的支持，且在保持高准确率和低延迟的同时实现FGAC存在固有技术难题。

Method: 通过形式化定义向量数据库中的FGAC模型及执行问题，比较多种执行策略，并通过初步研究分析实现可行性。

Result: 揭示了在保证ANN搜索高性能的同时实施FGAC的权衡关系，并总结了当前研究的初步发现及未来的开放性关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“策略感知向量搜索”（Policy-aware Vector Search）的愿景，形式化了向量数据库中细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的策略模型及执行问题，并指出了该领域面临的核心挑战。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [285] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对过滤式ANN搜索方法在不同场景下表现不一的问题，提出了一种查询感知的路由框架，利用轻量级ML模型动态选择最优算法，实现了高性能与高召回的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过滤式ANN（Filtered ANN）方法中没有单一方案在所有场景下占优，即使在同一数据集下，最优方法也会随查询变化，缺乏一种能根据查询特征动态选择最优执行策略的机制。

Method: 1. 建立包含多种类别过滤式ANN方法的基准测试表；2. 开发轻量级机器学习模型（使用回归方法）预测查询在各方法下的召回率；3. 基于预测的召回率和离线基准表，动态路由至最优的查询策略；4. 筛选出3个关键特征进行建模。

Result: 在5个未见过的验证数据集上，路由框架相比现有的过滤式ANN基线取得了最优的召回率与QPS平衡，同时保持了极低的查询延迟。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于查询感知的路由框架，通过机器学习模型和基准测试表，在多种过滤式ANN方法中选择最优方案，实现了性能与召回率的最佳平衡且几乎无额外延迟。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [286] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现代高速网络下内核TCP栈导致的数据库性能瓶颈，本文提出一种“双通道”协同设计方法，通过分流高性能数据传输（用户空间UDP）与可靠控制信息（内核TCP），在显著提升性能的同时兼顾可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高速现代云网络环境中，传统的内核TCP栈成为了数据库性能的瓶颈（CPU开销过大）；而直接使用用户空间UDP虽然高效却缺乏可靠性与顺序保证。

Method: 提出“双通道（Bi-channel）”范式：1. 数据通道（Data Path）：利用用户空间UDP进行高性能数据传输；2. 控制通道（Control Path）：利用内核TCP实现可靠的协调与恢复。该方案充分利用现代NIC功能，消除内核TCP栈在数据库通信中的开销。

Result: 在两个典型场景中验证了该方案：1. 分布式Shuffle：仅用3个CPU核即达到200 Gbit/s的吞吐量；2. 复制键值存储：实现了每秒处理数百万条消息的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将数据库操作与网络通信进行协同设计（Bi-channel范式），可以克服传统内核TCP栈在高速网络下的CPU开销瓶颈，实现极高的网络处理吞吐量，同时保持可靠性保证。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [287] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AgenticDB 是一个基于 Agent 的数据库重配置框架，通过实时系统交互、OS 级优化、安全约束和经验记忆机制，解决了传统调优方法空间受限和缺乏反馈诊断的问题，显著提升了数据库性能和调优效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化调优主要基于参数空间的迭代搜索，存在执行成本高、搜索空间通过预设严重受限，且在诊断运行时瓶颈、执行 OS 级配置、保障调优安全以及跨任务学习等方面能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出 AgenticDB 框架，采用基于上下文引导的 Agent 机制，与数据库环境进行实时交互。该方法包括：1. 涵盖 DBMS 和 OS 级别的参数调整空间；2. 实施安全约束以保障执行鲁棒性；3. 利用运行时状态反馈（性能指标和系统瓶颈）引导决策；4. 通过内置的记忆机制积累跨任务经验。

Result: 在 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 上的实验表明，相比最强的基线方法，AgenticDB 在所有测试负载中的最终性能平均提升了 118.1%，达到最佳性能的时间缩短了 22.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticDB 成功地将数据库调优转化为一种自我优化的重配置过程，通过结合 OS 级调优、安全约束机制和经验积累，显著提升了调优效率和性能表现，证明了基于 Agent 的方法在复杂数据库场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为“Physical Atari”的高性价比、高稳健性物理机器人强化学习平台，用于研究强化学习在真实环境中的部署挑战，并强调了在机器人任务中实现设备端自适应对性能的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究强化学习算法在物理世界中的表现及局限性，特别是在存在分布偏移（distribution shift）的情况下，需要一个能够进行长时间、高稳健性物理实验的经济型机器人研究平台。

Method: 构建了一套名为“Physical Atari”的硬件系统，包含操作雅达利手柄的机械臂（Robotroller）、渲染游戏画面的设备（Devbox）及计算机视觉系统；通过基于轴承设计的机械结构减少磨损，并引入高频伺服监控软件增强系统鲁棒性，同时结合低成本现成零件与3D打印制件实现高可访问性。

Result: 该系统成本低于1000美元，经受住了数周的不间断实验压力测试且无机械故障；实证研究表明强化学习算法可以直接在机器人上学习，但学习环境与部署环境间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: “物理雅达利”（Physical Atari）是一个经济、稳健且高可用的机器人强化学习评估平台，验证了在物理机器人上直接进行强化学习的可行性，并强调了设备端自适应（on-device adaptation）对于应对分布偏移、保障机器人复杂任务性能的核心作用。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [289] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推出了 WorkBenchMark，一个基于 LEGO Duplo 的机器人组装基准，通过 400 个任务评估机器人的操作与推理能力，并确立了非端到端的规划方案在复杂组装任务中的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端学习方法难以可靠地解决机器人组装中涉及的低级操作与高级符号推理相结合的问题，缺乏合适的基准来评估这些能力。

Method: 构建了基于 LEGO Duplo 的 400 个组装任务（涵盖四个复杂度等级）；提出了一种名为“Assembly-by-Disassembly”（先拆后组）的感知与规划基准方案。

Result: 所提出的基于规划的方案在所有任务复杂度等级上均超越了现代的端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。

Conclusion: WorkBenchMark 为机器人组装领域提供了一个标准化、多层级的评估框架，并证明了基于规划的方案在处理物理约束组装任务时优于目前的端到端视觉-语言-动作模型。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [290] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文是对3D场景图（3DSGs）的首个综合综述，通过形式化定义和系统性梳理，解决了领域内因标准不一导致的碎片化问题，并为未来研究指明了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图在机器人与计算机视觉领域应用广泛，但目前领域内缺乏统一的定义、构建方法和评估基准，导致不同研究间难以比较，限制了技术的鲁棒性与广泛应用。

Method: 对3D场景图（3DSGs）进行系统性综述：1. 形式化统一定义；2. 分析节点/边属性、层级结构及动态特性等建模选择；3. 梳理从原始观测到场景图的构建流程；4. 探讨下游任务及评估策略；5. 建立配套资源网站。

Result: 提供了一个全面的3D场景图知识图谱，总结了当前的主要建模范式、构建管道、评估指标及面临的开放性挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在通过统一的定义、建模框架和评估标准来整合碎片化的3D场景图研究，从而推动该领域向更鲁棒的现实应用发展。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others.
